{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01ECHY8bwW6MV1t9bC3u54He","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→```python\n     2→\"\"\"\n     3→FlockDesk – Social Workspace Orchestrator\n     4→-----------------------------------------\n     5→\n     6→core.ipc.event_bus\n     7→~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\n     8→\n     9→A lightweight, **process-safe** event-bus implementation that acts as the glue\n    10→between the independent micro-front-ends that compose the FlockDesk desktop\n    11→suite.  The implementation purposefully avoids external dependencies and\n    12→relies solely on the standard library (``asyncio``/``multiprocessing``) so that\n    13→plugins do not need to vendor additional wheels.\n    14→\n    15→Design goals\n    16→============\n    17→\n    18→1. Publish / subscribe with topic-based routing and Unix‐shell wild-card\n    19→   matching (``fnmatch``) so that a handler can listen to groups\n    20→   (e.g. ``\"presence.*\"``).\n    21→\n    22→2. Support both synchronous **and** asynchronous handlers without forcing plugin\n    23→   authors to learn ``asyncio``.\n    24→\n    25→3. Provide a *request / reply* pattern with correlation-IDs and automatic\n    26→   timeout handling—essential for client–server style interactions.\n    27→\n    28→4. Remain safe across processes.  A *broker* process (spawned lazily and managed\n    29→   via :pymod:`multiprocessing.managers`) forwards events between producer and\n    30→   consumer processes transparently.\n    31→\n    32→5. Offer graceful shutdown semantics so that the application can quit quickly\n    33→   even under heavy traffic.\n    34→\n    35→The broker architecture may feel heavyweight for local desktop IPC, but it\n    36→guarantees that crashing micro-front-ends cannot bring down the other windows.\n    37→\"\"\"\n    38→\n    39→from __future__ import annotations\n    40→\n    41→import asyncio\n    42→import contextlib\n    43→import fnmatch\n    44→import inspect\n    45→import logging\n    46→import queue\n    47→import threading\n    48→import time\n    49→import uuid\n    50→from dataclasses import dataclass, field\n    51→from multiprocessing import Event as MpEvent\n    52→from multiprocessing import Process, Queue\n    53→from multiprocessing.managers import BaseManager\n    54→from types import TracebackType\n    55→from typing import (\n    56→    Any,\n    57→    Awaitable,\n    58→    Callable,\n    59→    Dict,\n    60→    List,\n    61→    Mapping,\n    62→    MutableMapping,\n    63→    Optional,\n    64→    Tuple,\n    65→    Type,\n    66→    Union,\n    67→)\n    68→\n    69→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    70→# Logging\n    71→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    72→_log = logging.getLogger(\"flockdesk.ipc.event_bus\")\n    73→_log.addHandler(logging.NullHandler())\n    74→\n    75→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    76→# Exceptions\n    77→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    78→\n    79→\n    80→class EventBusError(RuntimeError):\n    81→    \"\"\"Base-class for all event-bus exceptions.\"\"\"\n    82→\n    83→\n    84→class HandlerTimeout(EventBusError):\n    85→    \"\"\"Raised by ``request`` when no reply arrived in time.\"\"\"\n    86→\n    87→\n    88→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    89→# Event primitives\n    90→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    91→\n    92→\n    93→@dataclass(slots=True, frozen=True)\n    94→class Event:\n    95→    \"\"\"\n    96→    A single immutable message traveling through the *in-memory* event bus.\n    97→\n    98→    Attributes\n    99→    ----------\n   100→    topic:\n   101→        Hierarchical, ``dot``-separated topic name\n   102→        (e.g. ``\"presence.updated\"``).\n   103→    payload:\n   104→        Opaque data expected by subscribers.  Must be *pickleable* because the\n   105→        broker relies on :pymod:`multiprocessing.Queue`.\n   106→    headers:\n   107→        Optional free-form metadata such as authentication tokens or tracing\n   108→        information.  Header values must again be pickleable.\n   109→    event_id:\n   110→        Unique identifier for tracing/debugging purposes.\n   111→    timestamp:\n   112→        Epoch seconds (float) at creation time.\n   113→    origin_pid:\n   114→        The operating-system process identifier of the sender.  Helpful when the\n   115→        same user runs multiple instances of FlockDesk on the same machine.\n   116→    \"\"\"\n   117→\n   118→    topic: str\n   119→    payload: Any\n   120→    headers: Mapping[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)\n   121→    event_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: uuid.uuid4().hex, init=False)\n   122→    timestamp: float = field(default_factory=time.time, init=False)\n   123→    origin_pid: int = field(default_factory=lambda: threading.get_native_id(), init=False)\n   124→\n   125→    def __post_init__(self) -> None:  # noqa: D401\n   126→        \"\"\"Validate that the topic is non-empty.\"\"\"\n   127→        if not self.topic or not isinstance(self.topic, str):\n   128→            raise ValueError(\"Event.topic must be a non-empty string\")\n   129→\n   130→\n   131→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   132→# Subscription Handle\n   133→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   134→\n   135→\n   136→class _Subscription:\n   137→    \"\"\"\n   138→    Lightweight handle that allows clients to stop listening without keeping\n   139→    implementation details around.  Instances can also be used as *async*\n   140→    context-managers.\n   141→\n   142→    NOTE: Users are **not** expected to instantiate this class directly.\n   143→    \"\"\"\n   144→\n   145→    __slots__ = (\"_topic\", \"_handler\", \"_bus\", \"_active\")\n   146→\n   147→    def __init__(\n   148→        self,\n   149→        topic: str,\n   150→        handler: \"Handler\",\n   151→        bus: \"EventBus\",\n   152→    ) -> None:\n   153→        self._topic = topic\n   154→        self._handler = handler\n   155→        self._bus = bus\n   156→        self._active = True\n   157→\n   158→    async def __aenter__(self) -> \"_Subscription\":\n   159→        return self\n   160→\n   161→    async def __aexit__(\n   162→        self,\n   163→        exc_type: Optional[Type[BaseException]],\n   164→        exc: Optional[BaseException],\n   165→        tb: Optional[TracebackType],\n   166→    ) -> None:\n   167→        self.unsubscribe()\n   168→\n   169→    def unsubscribe(self) -> None:\n   170→        \"\"\"Remove the handler from the bus.\"\"\"\n   171→        if self._active:\n   172→            self._bus._remove_handler(self._topic, self._handler)  # noqa: SLF001\n   173→            self._active = False\n   174→\n   175→\n   176→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   177→# Type aliases\n   178→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   179→\n   180→SyncHandler = Callable[[Event], None]\n   181→AsyncHandler = Callable[[Event], Awaitable[None]]\n   182→Handler = Union[SyncHandler, AsyncHandler]\n   183→\n   184→# Internal maps\n   185→_HandlerMap = Dict[str, List[Handler]]\n   186→\n   187→\n   188→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   189→# Broker (separate process)\n   190→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   191→\n   192→# Queues are *multiprocessing* queues, therefore pickle based and safe between\n   193→# forked/spawned processes.\n   194→_InboundQueue = Queue  # alias for readability\n   195→_OutboundQueue = Queue\n   196→\n   197→\n   198→class _Broker(Process):\n   199→    \"\"\"\n   200→    Spin-up a separate process that forwards a single *inbound* queue to many\n   201→    *outbound* queues (fan-out).\n   202→\n   203→    Each process that calls ``EventBus.connect()`` will create its own outbound\n   204→    queue, while the producing bus writes to a single inbound queue.\n   205→\n   206→    Diagram::\n   207→\n   208→        ┌────────────────────┐\n   209→        │  client process A  │──┐\n   210→        └────────────────────┘  │  ┌────────────┐\n   211→                                ├─▶│            │\n   212→        ┌────────────────────┐  │  │            │\n   213→        │  client process B  │──┼─▶│   BROKER   │──▶ Inbound\n   214→        └────────────────────┘  │  │            │    Queue\n   215→                                └─▶│            │\n   216→                                   └────────────┘\n   217→\n   218→    \"\"\"\n   219→\n   220→    def __init__(self, inbound: _InboundQueue, shutdown_evt: MpEvent):\n   221→        super().__init__(name=\"FlockDeskEventBroker\", daemon=True)\n   222→        self._inbound: _InboundQueue = inbound\n   223→        self._shutdown_evt: MpEvent = shutdown_evt\n   224→        self._outbounds: List[_OutboundQueue] = []\n   225→\n   226→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   227→    # Public API for other processes (via SyncManager)\n   228→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   229→    def register_consumer(self) -> _OutboundQueue:\n   230→        \"\"\"\n   231→        Called by a client process to obtain a dedicated outbound queue.\n   232→        \"\"\"\n   233→        q: _OutboundQueue = Queue()\n   234→        self._outbounds.append(q)\n   235→        _log.debug(\"Broker registered new consumer queue (total=%d)\", len(self._outbounds))\n   236→        return q\n   237→\n   238→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   239→    # Main loop\n   240→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   241→    def run(self) -> None:  # noqa: D401\n   242→        _log.info(\"Event-bus broker running (PID=%d)\", self.pid)\n   243→        try:\n   244→            while not self._shutdown_evt.is_set():\n   245→                try:\n   246→                    evt: Event = self._inbound.get(timeout=0.1)\n   247→                except queue.Empty:\n   248→                    continue\n   249→\n   250→                # Broadcast\n   251→                for q in self._outbounds:\n   252→                    try:\n   253→                        q.put_nowait(evt)\n   254→                    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n   255→                        _log.exception(\"Failed to forward event to queue %s\", q)\n   256→        finally:\n   257→            _log.info(\"Event-bus broker shutting down (PID=%d)\", self.pid)\n   258→\n   259→\n   260→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   261→# Multiprocessing manager that exposes broker methods\n   262→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   263→\n   264→\n   265→class _BusManager(BaseManager):\n   266→    pass\n   267→\n   268→\n   269→# have to register after class definition\n   270→_BusManager.register(\"register_consumer\", callable=None)  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n   271→\n   272→\n   273→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   274→# EventBus (public)\n   275→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   276→class EventBus:\n   277→    \"\"\"\n   278→    Public entry point used by every micro-front-end.\n   279→\n   280→    Processes talk to a shared broker, threads inside the same process share\n   281→    exactly one :class:`EventBus` instance (thread-safe singleton).\n   282→    \"\"\"\n   283→\n   284→    _singleton: \"EventBus\" | None = None\n   285→    _singleton_lock = threading.Lock()\n   286→\n   287→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   288→    # Construction helpers\n   289→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   290→    @classmethod\n   291→    def connect(cls) -> \"EventBus\":\n   292→        \"\"\"\n   293→        Return a *singleton* instance of :class:`EventBus` for the current\n   294→        process.  Lazily starts the broker **once** per workstation.\n   295→        \"\"\"\n   296→        with cls._singleton_lock:\n   297→            if cls._singleton is None:\n   298→                cls._singleton = cls._create()\n   299→        return cls._singleton\n   300→\n   301→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   302→    # Instance-level lifecycle\n   303→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   304→    @classmethod\n   305→    def _create(cls) -> \"EventBus\":\n   306→        # 1. Spawn or attach to broker\n   307→        inbound: _InboundQueue = Queue()\n   308→        shutdown_evt: MpEvent = MpEvent()\n   309→\n   310→        broker = _Broker(inbound, shutdown_evt)\n   311→        broker.start()\n   312→\n   313→        # 2. Register via SyncManager so that we can obtain our *private* queue\n   314→        manager = _BusManager(\n   315→            address=(\"localhost\", 0), authkey=uuid.uuid4().hex.encode(\"ascii\")\n   316→        )\n   317→        # The manager itself runs in a sub-process—start it:\n   318→        manager.start()\n   319→        # Now we can register the broker method for remote access\n   320→        manager.register(\"register_consumer\", broker.register_consumer)  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n   321→\n   322→        consumer_q: _OutboundQueue = manager.register_consumer()  # type: ignore[attr-defined]\n   323→\n   324→        return cls(\n   325→            inbound=inbound,\n   326→            outbound=consumer_q,\n   327→            shutdown_evt=shutdown_evt,\n   328→            broker=broker,\n   329→            manager=manager,\n   330→        )\n   331→\n   332→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   333→    # dunder\n   334→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   335→    def __init__(\n   336→        self,\n   337→        *,\n   338→        inbound: _InboundQueue,\n   339→        outbound: _OutboundQueue,\n   340→        shutdown_evt: MpEvent,\n   341→        broker: _Broker,\n   342→        manager: _BusManager,\n   343→    ) -> None:\n   344→        self._inbound = inbound\n   345→        self._outbound = outbound\n   346→        self._broker = broker\n   347→        self._shutdown_evt = shutdown_evt\n   348→        self._manager = manager\n   349→\n   350→        self._handlers: _HandlerMap = {}\n   351→        self._loop = asyncio.get_event_loop_policy().get_event_loop()\n   352→        self._dispatch_task: asyncio.Task[None] | None = None\n   353→\n   354→        # Protect _handlers as multiple event-loop threads could touch it\n   355→        self._h_lock = asyncio.Lock()\n   356→\n   357→        # Synchronize start\n   358→        self._ensure_dispatcher()\n   359→\n   360→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   361→    # Public API\n   362→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   363→    def subscribe(self, topic: str, handler: Handler) -> _Subscription:\n   364→        \"\"\"\n   365→        Register *handler* for the given *topic* pattern.\n   366→\n   367→        Wildcards: Use ``'*'`` to match one segment, ``'**'`` to match\n   368→        anything recursively (see :pymod:`fnmatch`).\n   369→\n   370→        Returns\n   371→        -------\n   372→        _Subscription\n   373→            A tiny handle that can be used to *unsubscribe* again.\n   374→        \"\"\"\n   375→        if not callable(handler):\n   376→            raise TypeError(\"Handler must be callable\")\n   377→\n   378→        _log.debug(\"Subscribing %s to topic=%s\", handler, topic)\n   379→\n   380→        # Keep sync handler as is, wrap non-coroutine functions so that we can\n   381→        # `await` them uniformly.\n   382→        if not inspect.iscoroutinefunction(handler):\n   383→\n   384→            async def _async_adapter(evt: Event, _h=handler) -> None:\n   385→                await self._loop.run_in_executor(None, _h, evt)\n   386→\n   387→            adapted: Handler = _async_adapter\n   388→        else:\n   389→            adapted = handler  # type: ignore[assignment]\n   390→\n   391→        # Insert\n   392→        async def _insert() -> None:\n   393→            async with self._h_lock:\n   394→                self._handlers.setdefault(topic, []).append(adapted)\n   395→\n   396→        asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(_insert(), self._loop).result()\n   397→        return _Subscription(topic, adapted, self)\n   398→\n   399→    def publish(self, topic: str, payload: Any, headers: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None) -> None:\n   400→        \"\"\"\n   401→        Fire-and-forget publish.  Safe to call from **any** thread—will enqueue\n   402→        the event into the broker’s *inbound* queue.\n   403→        \"\"\"\n   404→        evt = Event(topic=topic, payload=payload, headers=headers or {})\n   405→        try:\n   406→            self._inbound.put_nowait(evt)\n   407→        except Exception as exc:  # Local handler just completes the future\n   431→        async def _reply_handler(evt: Event) -> None:\n   432→            fut.set_result(evt)\n   433→\n   434→        subscription = self.subscribe(reply_topic, _reply_handler)\n   435→\n   436→        # Publish *request*\n   437→        hdrs: Dict[str, Any] = dict(headers or {})\n   438→        hdrs[\"reply_to\"] = reply_topic\n   439→        hdrs[\"correlation_id\"] = correlation_id\n   440→        self.publish(topic, payload, hdrs)\n   441→\n   442→        try:\n   443→            return await asyncio.wait_for(fut, timeout=timeout)\n   444→        except asyncio.TimeoutError as exc:\n   445→            raise HandlerTimeout(f\"Request timed out after {timeout}s\") from exc\n   446→        finally:\n   447→            subscription.unsubscribe()\n   448→\n   449→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   450→    # Shutdown\n   451→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   452→    def close(self) -> None:\n   453→        \"\"\"\n   454→        Close the bus, stop the background tasks and terminate the broker.\n   455→\n   456→        **Important:** This method should be called exactly *once* at\n   457→        application shutdown.\n   458→        \"\"\"\n   459→        _log.info(\"Shutting down EventBus in process=%d\", threading.get_native_id())\n   460→\n   461→        self._shutdown_evt.set()\n   462→\n   463→        if self._dispatch_task:\n   464→            self._dispatch_task.cancel()\n   465→\n   466→        # Give the broker a moment to finish pending broadcasts\n   467→        if self._broker.is_alive():\n   468→            self._broker.join(timeout=1.0)\n   469→        self._manager.shutdown()\n   470→\n   471→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   472→    # Internal helpers\n   473→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   474→    def _remove_handler(self, topic: str, handler: Handler) -> None:\n   475→        async def _remove() -> None:\n   476→            async with self._h_lock:\n   477→                lst = self._handlers.get(topic, [])\n   478→                if handler in lst:\n   479→                    lst.remove(handler)\n   480→\n   481→        asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(_remove(), self._loop).result()\n   482→\n   483→    def _ensure_dispatcher(self) -> None:\n   484→        if self._dispatch_task is None or self._dispatch_task.done():\n   485→            self._dispatch_task = self._loop.create_task(self._dispatcher())\n   486→\n   487→    async def _dispatcher(self) -> None:\n   488→        \"\"\"\n   489→        Background coroutine that consumes events from the *outbound* queue and\n   490→        delivers them to pattern-matched handlers.\n   491→        \"\"\"\n   492→        _log.debug(\"EventBus dispatcher started (thread=%s)\", threading.current_thread().name)\n   493→        while not self._shutdown_evt.is_set():\n   494→            try:\n   495→                # Non-blocking read in *executor* because Queue.get is blocking but\n   496→                # not async aware.\n   497→                evt: Event = await self._loop.run_in_executor(None, self._outbound.get, True, 0.1)\n   498→            except queue.Empty:\n   499→                continue\n   500→            except Exception:  # Flatten into (topic_pattern, handlers[])\n   510→            items: List[Tuple[str, List[Handler]]] = list(self._handlers.items())\n   511→\n   512→        coros: List[Awaitable[None]] = []\n   513→\n   514→        for pattern, handlers in items:\n   515→            if fnmatch.fnmatchcase(evt.topic, pattern):\n   516→                coros.extend(h(evt) for h in handlers)\n   517→\n   518→        if not coros:\n   519→            return  # no listeners\n   520→\n   521→        await asyncio.gather(*coros, return_exceptions=False)\n   522→\n   523→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   524→    # Context manager sugar\n   525→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   526→    def __enter__(self) -> \"EventBus\":\n   527→        return self\n   528→\n   529→    def __exit__(\n   530→        self,\n   531→        exc_type: Optional[Type[BaseException]],\n   532→        exc: Optional[BaseException],\n   533→        tb: Optional[TracebackType],\n   534→    ) -> None:\n   535→        self.close()\n   536→\n   537→\n   538→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   539→# Convenience helpers for library-wide singleton access\n   540→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   541→\n   542→_bus: EventBus | None = None\n   543→_bus_lock = threading.Lock()\n   544→\n   545→\n   546→def get_event_bus() -> EventBus:\n   547→    \"\"\"\n   548→    Return a process-global :class:`EventBus` instance, creating it on first use.\n   549→    \"\"\"\n   550→    global _bus\n   551→    with _bus_lock:\n   552→        if _bus is None:\n   553→            _bus = EventBus.connect()\n   554→        return _bus\n   555→\n   556→\n   557→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   558→# Example usage (guarded)\n   559→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   560→if __name__ == \"__main__\":  #    \"\"\"\n   127→        Send a message to the current channel.\n   128→\n   129→        The actual network interaction is delegated to the domain layer\n   130→        via the event-bus. Each card is a plugin that exposes a QWidget\n     8→and registers itself to the internal event-bus.  Users can re-arrange,\n     9→remove, and add cards simply by dragging & dropping new plugins from the\n    10→plugin-palette or by right-clicking a card and selecting *Remove*.\n    11→\n    12→Design goals\n    13→------------\n    14→1. Plugin friendly – cards are loaded/unloaded via the PluginManager.\n    18→4. Let me create a detailed analysis document.\n\n## FlockDesk Codebase Architecture Analysis - Focus Mode Implementation Guide\n\nBased on my thorough exploration of the FlockDesk codebase, here's the complete architectural analysis you need to implement Focus Mode correctly:\n\n---\n\n### 1. **EventBus Implementation** (`/app/project/flockdesk/core/ipc/event_bus.py`)\n\n**Architecture:**\n- **Process-Safe Design**: Uses multiprocessing queues and a broker process (separate OS process)\n- **Broker Pattern**: Fan-out architecture where one inbound queue receives events and broadcasts to multiple outbound queues (one per connected process)\n- **Async-First**: Built on `asyncio` with support for both sync and async handlers\n\n**Key Classes:**\n- `Event`: Immutable dataclass with topic, payload, headers, event_id, timestamp, origin_pid\n- `_Broker`: Separate OS process that forwards events from inbound to multiple outbound queues\n- `EventBus`: Main singleton per-process with thread-safe subscription management\n- `_Subscription`: Handle allowing unsubscribe with context manager support\n\n**Public API:**\n```python\n# Singleton access\nbus = EventBus.connect()  # Creates broker once per workstation\n\n# Subscribe (sync or async handlers - both supported)\nsubscription = bus.subscribe(topic=\"chat.message.*\", handler=my_handler)\n\n# Publish (fire-and-forget)\nbus.publish(topic=\"my.topic\", payload={\"data\": ...})\n\n# Request-Reply pattern with timeout\nreply = await bus.request(topic=\"command.topic\", payload=..., timeout=5.0)\n\n# Cleanup\nbus.close()\n```\n\n**Critical Patterns for Focus Mode:**\n- Use wildcard patterns: `\"focus.*\"` matches `\"focus.enabled\"`, `\"focus.mode.changed\"`, etc.\n- Both sync and async handlers are wrapped uniformly via asyncio executor\n- Events are pickled across process boundaries\n- Unsubscribe must be explicit via returned `_Subscription` object\n\n---\n\n### 2. **Event Types & Registry** (`/app/project/flockdesk/core/ipc/event_types.py`)\n\n**Architecture:**\n- **Type-Safe Events**: Dataclass-based payloads with automatic registration\n- **Registry Pattern**: Single global `EventRegistry()` tracks all known events by FQN\n- **Decorator Pattern**: `@event` decorator registers payload types at module import time\n\n**Key Components:**\n```python\nclass EventTopic(str, Enum):\n    CHAT = \"chat\"\n    SETTINGS = \"settings\"\n    PRESENCE = \"presence\"\n    SYSTEM = \"system\"\n    # etc.\n\n@dataclass(slots=True, frozen=True)\nclass Event:\n    topic: EventTopic\n    name: str  # hierarchical: \"message.sent\", \"mode.changed\"\n    payload: PayloadT\n    timestamp, correlation_id, reply_to  # automatic\n\n@event(topic=EventTopic.SETTINGS, name=\"changed\")\n@dataclass(slots=True, frozen=True)\nclass SettingsChanged(_PayloadBase):\n    user_id: str\n    key: str\n    old_value: Any\n    new_value: Any\n    changed_at: datetime\n```\n\n**For Focus Mode:**\n- Create event definitions in a new topic or under SETTINGS\n- Payload must be frozen dataclass implementing `Mapping[str, Any]`\n- FQN example: `\"settings.focus_mode_changed\"` for `EventTopic.SETTINGS`\n\n---\n\n### 3. **SettingsService** (`/app/project/flockdesk/core/services/settings_service.py`)\n\n**Architecture:**\n- **Namespace-Based**: Multiple independent settings buckets (one per plugin/domain)\n- **Schema-Driven**: Strongly-typed via dataclasses, JSON serialization/deserialization\n- **Atomic Persistence**: Writes to `.tmp` then renames (atomic on all OSes)\n- **Event Broadcasting**: Changes published to event-bus automatically\n- **Autosave Thread**: Background thread with debouncing (5-second intervals)\n\n**Key Classes:**\n```python\nclass SettingsService:\n    def register_schema(self, namespace: str, schema_cls: Type[T]) -> None\n    def get(self, namespace: str) -> Any  # Returns MappingProxy (immutable)\n    def set_value(self, namespace: str, key: str, value: Any) -> None\n    def edit(self, namespace: str) -> TransientOverride  # Context manager\n    def shutdown() -> None\n\n# Decorator for cleaner registration\n@register_settings_schema(\"focus_mode\")\n@dataclass\nclass FocusModeSettings:\n    enabled: bool = False\n    mode: str = \"strict\"  # or \"relaxed\", etc.\n```\n\n**For Focus Mode:**\n- Register schema once at module initialization\n- Settings auto-broadcast to event-bus after `set_value()`\n- Use `TransientOverride` for preview-mode UI interactions\n- Thread-safe with RLock protection on all operations\n\n**Default Config** (`/app/project/configs/default_settings.json`):\n- Top-level structure: feature flags under `\"experiments\"` or `\"gui\"` sections\n- JSON schema for validation/documentation\n\n---\n\n### 4. **Shortcuts/Commands System** (`/app/project/flockdesk/core/shortcuts/commands.py`)\n\n**Architecture:**\n- **Command Pattern**: Encapsulates actions as first-class objects\n- **Singleton Registry**: Thread-safe with RLock, supports hot-reload\n- **Qt Integration**: Emits Qt signals on registration/trigger/error\n- **Persistence**: Overrides persisted in settings, default in schema\n\n**Key Classes:**\n```python\n@dataclass(slots=True)\nclass ShortcutCommand:\n    id: str  # e.g., \"core.focus_mode.toggle\"\n    description: str\n    default_sequence: QKeySequence  # e.g., \"Ctrl+Shift+F\"\n    handler: Callable[[], None]\n    enabled: Callable[[], bool] = lambda: True\n    owner: Optional[str] = None\n    current_sequence: QKeySequence  # Runtime, auto-populated from settings\n\n    def is_enabled(self) -> bool\n    def trigger() -> None  # Executes handler, emits events\n\nclass ShortcutCommandRegistry(QObject):  # Singleton\n    command_registered = Signal(str)  # command-id\n    command_triggered = Signal(str)\n    \n    def register(self, id, description, sequence, handler, ...) -> ShortcutCommand\n    def trigger(self, id: str) -> None\n    def update_binding(self, id: str, sequence: str|QKeySequence) -> None\n    def materialise_for_widget(self, widget, context) -> List[QShortcut]\n```\n\n**For Focus Mode:**\n```python\n# Register at module init\nregistry = ShortcutCommandRegistry()\nregistry.register(\n    id=\"focus_mode.toggle\",\n    description=\"Toggle Focus Mode\",\n    sequence=\"Ctrl+Shift+F\",\n    handler=lambda: bus.publish(\"focus.toggle_requested\", {}),\n    owner=\"flockdesk.core\"\n)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 5. **Menu Bar** (`/app/project/flockdesk/core/shell/menu_bar.py`)\n\n**Architecture:**\n- **Protocol-Based**: Loosely coupled to plugin registry, theme manager, settings\n- **Hot-Reload Aware**: Listens to plugin loaded/unloaded signals\n- **Exception-Safe**: `_SafeSlot` wrapper prevents handler crashes from propagating\n- **Background Tasks**: Uses `QThreadPool` for expensive operations\n\n**Key Pattern:**\n```python\nclass MenuBar(QMenuBar):\n    def __init__(self, parent, *, event_bus, plugin_registry, \n                 theme_manager, settings_facade):\n        # Wires connections, populates static structure\n        self._init_static_structure()  # File, Edit, View, Plugins, Help\n        self._populate_dynamic_sections()  # Themes, plugin contributions\n        \n        # Listen for plugin lifecycle\n        self._plugins.on_plugin_loaded(self._on_plugin_loaded)\n```\n\n**For Focus Mode:**\n- Add menu item under \"View\" or new \"Tools\" menu\n- Wire action to publish event on event-bus\n- Use `_SafeSlot` wrapper to prevent crashes\n\n---\n\n### 6. **MVVM Pattern Used Throughout**\n\n**Pattern Structure:**\n1. **ViewModel (QObject)**: \n   - Holds presentation state + Qt signals\n   - Subscribes to event-bus\n   - Exposes command API (methods for controller)\n   - Emits signals when state changes\n\n2. **View (QWidget)**:\n   - Connected to ViewModel via Qt signals/slots\n   - Renders current state\n   - Publishes user actions back to ViewModel methods\n   - Unsubscribes from event-bus on close\n\n3. **Service Layer**:\n   - Domain logic, event-bus subscriptions\n   - Async operations via asyncio\n\n**Chat ViewModel Example** (`/app/project/flockdesk/modules/chat/viewmodel/chat_vm.py`):\n```python\nclass ChatViewModel(QtCore.QObject):\n    # Qt Signals (emit when state changes)\n    messageAdded = QtCore.Signal(ChatMessage)\n    typingStatusChanged = QtCore.Signal(str, bool)\n    errorOccurred = QtCore.Signal(str)\n    \n    def __init__(self, channel_id: str, event_bus: EventBus, parent=None):\n        super().__init__(parent)\n        self._event_bus = event_bus\n        # Subscribe to domain events\n        self._register_event_subscriptions()\n    \n    # Public API for controller\n    def send_message(self, content: str) -> None:\n        # Optimistic update\n        msg = ChatMessage(...)\n        self._append_message(msg)\n        # Publish to domain layer\n        self._event_bus.publish(Event(topic=\"chat.outgoing\", payload=...))\n    \n    # Event handlers (called by event-bus)\n    def _on_incoming_message(self, event: Event) -> None:\n        msg = ChatMessage(...)\n        self._append_message(msg)\n        self.messageAdded.emit(msg)  # Signal to view\n```\n\n**Chat View Example** (`/app/project/flockdesk/modules/chat/view/chat_widget.py`):\n```python\nclass ChatWidget(QtWidgets.QWidget):\n    def __init__(self, view_model: ChatViewModel, event_bus: EventBus):\n        self._vm = view_model\n        self._bus = event_bus\n        self._setup_ui()\n        self._connect_signals()\n    \n    def _connect_signals(self):\n        # ViewModel → View updates\n        self._vm.messageAdded.connect(self._scroll_to_bottom)\n        # View → ViewModel actions\n        self._send_btn.clicked.connect(self._on_send_clicked)\n    \n    @QtCore.Slot()\n    def _on_send_clicked(self):\n        text = self._input_box.toPlainText()\n        self._vm.send_message(text)  # Call ViewModel method\n```\n\n---\n\n### 7. **Dashboard Module Structure** (`/app/project/flockdesk/modules/dashboard/`)\n\n**Organization:**\n```\ndashboard/\n├── main.py                    # Entry point, plugin registration\n├── service.py                 # DashboardService (domain logic)\n├── viewmodel/\n│   └── dashboard_vm.py       # DashboardViewModel (MVVM, Qt signals)\n├── view/\n│   └── dashboard_widget.py   # DashboardWidget (Qt UI)\n└── model/\n    └── (data models)\n```\n\n**Dashboard ViewModel** (`dashboard_vm.py`):\n```python\nclass DashboardViewModel(QObject):\n    # Signals\n    widgetsChanged = Signal(list)      # List[DashboardWidget]\n    themeChanged = Signal(str)\n    errorOccurred = Signal(str)\n    \n    # Commands (public API)\n    _commands = {\n        \"addWidget\": self.add_widget,\n        \"removeWidget\": self.remove_widget,\n        \"switchTheme\": self.switch_theme,\n    }\n    \n    def run_command(self, name: str, *args, **kwargs):\n        return self._commands[name](*args, **kwargs)\n    \n    def _register_event_listeners(self):\n        self._subscriptions.extend([\n            self._event_bus.subscribe(\"plugin.loaded\", ...),\n            self._event_bus.subscribe(\"theme.external_switch\", ...),\n        ])\n```\n\n**Dashboard Service** (`service.py`):\n- Orchestrates widget lifecycle\n- Persists layout to JSON\n- Hot-reload support\n- Event-bus bridging\n\n---\n\n### 8. **Integration Points for Focus Mode**\n\n**Strategy: Implement as cross-cutting concern following these patterns:**\n\n#### A.     self._bus.subscribe(\"focus.toggle_requested\", self._on_toggle_requested)\n        # Subscribe to settings changes\n        self._bus.subscribe(\"settings.updated\", self._on_settings_changed)\n    \n    def _on_toggle_requested(self, event: Event) -> None:\n        settings = self._settings.get(\"focus_mode\")\n        new_state = not settings[\"enabled\"]\n        self._settings.set_value(\"focus_mode\", \"enabled\", new_state)\n        \n        # Publish state change\n        self._bus.publish(\n            Event(\n                topic=\"settings\",\n                name=\"focus_mode.changed\",\n                payload=FocusModeChanged(enabled=new_state, ...)\n            )\n        )\n    \n    def _on_settings_changed(self, event: Event) -> None:\n        if event.payload.get(\"namespace\") == \"focus_mode\":\n            # Apply focus mode: hide modules, disable notifications, etc.\n            settings = self._settings.get(\"focus_mode\")\n            if settings[\"enabled\"]:\n                self._apply_focus_mode(settings)\n            else:\n                self._disable_focus_mode()\n```\n\n---\n\n### 9. **Key Architectural Patterns Summary**\n\n| Pattern | Where Used | Why | Implementation |\n|---------|-----------|-----|-----------------|\n| **Event-Driven** | EventBus, services | Decoupling, async support | Pub/sub with wildcard routing |\n| **MVVM** | All modules (chat, dashboard) | Separation of concerns, testability | QObject + Qt signals |\n| **Singleton** | EventBus, SettingsService, Registry | Global state management | Thread-safe lazy initialization |\n| **Registry** | CommandRegistry, EventRegistry | Plugin extensibility | Dynamic registration at runtime |\n| **Protocol/Interface** | Menu, commands, theme manager | Loose coupling | Python Protocol types |\n| **Context Manager** | SettingsService (TransientOverride) | Resource cleanup, testing | `__enter__/__exit__` |\n| **Broker** | EventBus | Multi-process safety | Separate OS process fan-out |\n| **Decorator** | `@event`, `@register_settings_schema` | Ergonomic registration | Type-safe registration at module load |\n\n---\n\n### 10. be pickleable\n3. **Async Integration**: SettingsService runs in background thread; don't block UI\n4. **Event Ordering**: No guaranteed ordering between processes; use correlation_ids for request-reply\n5. **Persistence**: SettingsService auto-persists to `~/.flockdesk/focus_mode.json` (appdirs)\n6. **Hot-Reload**: Commands can be re-registered; events can be added at runtime via decorator\n7. **Error Handling**: All event handlers wrapped in exception-safe slots; exceptions logged but don't crash\n8. **Memory**: Subscriptions must be explicit; unsubscribe to avoid leaks Qt Signals (emit when state changes)\n    messageAdded = QtCore.Signal(ChatMessage)\n    typingStatusChanged = QtCore.Signal(str, bool)\n    errorOccurred = QtCore.Signal(str)\n    \n    def __init__(self, channel_id: str, event_bus: EventBus, parent=None):\n        super().__init__(parent)\n        self._event_bus = event_bus\n        # Subscribe to domain events\n        self._register_event_subscriptions()\n    \n    # Public API for controller\n    def send_message(self, content: str) -> None:\n        # Optimistic update\n        msg = ChatMessage(...)\n        self._append_message(msg)\n        # Publish to domain layer\n        self._event_bus.publish(Event(topic=\"chat.outgoing\", payload=...))\n    \n    # Event handlers (called by event-bus)\n    def _on_incoming_message(self, event: Event) -> None:\n        msg = ChatMessage(...)\n        self._append_message(msg)\n        self.messageAdded.emit(msg)  # Signal to view\n```\n\n**Chat View Example** (`/app/project/flockdesk/modules/chat/view/chat_widget.py`):\n```python\nclass ChatWidget(QtWidgets.QWidget):\n    def __init__(self, view_model: ChatViewModel, event_bus: EventBus):\n        self._vm = view_model\n        self._bus = event_bus\n        self._setup_ui()\n        self._connect_signals()\n    \n    def _connect_signals(self):\n        # ViewModel → View updates\n        self._vm.messageAdded.connect(self._scroll_to_bottom)\n        # View → ViewModel actions\n        self._send_btn.clicked.connect(self._on_send_clicked)\n    \n    @QtCore.Slot()\n    def _on_send_clicked(self):\n        text = self._input_box.toPlainText()\n        self._vm.send_message(text)  # Call ViewModel method\n```\n\n---\n\n### 7. **Dashboard Module Structure** (`/app/project/flockdesk/modules/dashboard/`)\n\n**Organization:**\n```\ndashboard/\n├── main.py                    # Entry point, plugin registration\n├── service.py                 # DashboardService (domain logic)\n├── viewmodel/\n│   └── dashboard_vm.py       # DashboardViewModel (MVVM, Qt signals)\n├── view/\n│   └── dashboard_widget.py   # DashboardWidget (Qt UI)\n└── model/\n    └── (data models)\n```\n\n**Dashboard ViewModel** (`dashboard_vm.py`):\n```python\nclass DashboardViewModel(QObject):\n    # Signals\n    widgetsChanged = Signal(list)      # List[DashboardWidget]\n    themeChanged = Signal(str)\n    errorOccurred = Signal(str)\n    \n    # Commands (public API)\n    _commands = {\n        \"addWidget\": self.add_widget,\n        \"removeWidget\": self.remove_widget,\n        \"switchTheme\": self.switch_theme,\n    }\n    \n    def run_command(self, name: str, *args, **kwargs):\n        return self._commands[name](*args, **kwargs)\n    \n    def _register_event_listeners(self):\n        self._subscriptions.extend([\n            self._event_bus.subscribe(\"plugin.loaded\", ...),\n            self._event_bus.subscribe(\"theme.external_switch\", ...),\n        ])\n```\n\n**Dashboard Service** (`service.py`):\n- Orchestrates widget lifecycle\n- Persists layout to JSON\n- Hot-reload support\n- Event-bus bridging\n\n---\n\n### 8. **Integration Points for Focus Mode**\n\n**Strategy: Implement as cross-cutting concern following these patterns:**\n\n#### A. Settings Integration\n```python\n# Create namespace\n@register_settings_schema(\"focus_mode\")\n@dataclass\nclass FocusModeSettings:\n    enabled: bool = False\n    strict_mode: bool = False\n    hide_chat: bool = True\n    hide_whiteboard: bool = False\n    duration_minutes: int = 25\n```\n\n#### B. Event Types\n```python\n# Add to event_types.py\n@event(topic=EventTopic.SETTINGS, name=\"focus_mode.changed\")\n@dataclass(slots=True, frozen=True)\nclass FocusModeChanged(_PayloadBase):\n    enabled: bool\n    mode: str  # \"strict\", \"relaxed\"\n    changed_at: datetime\n```\n\n#### C. Command Registration\n```python\n# Register in shortcuts/commands.py or module init\nregistry.register(\n    id=\"focus_mode.toggle\",\n    description=\"Toggle Focus Mode\",\n    sequence=\"Ctrl+Shift+F\",\n    handler=toggle_focus_mode,\n    owner=\"flockdesk.core\"\n)\n```\n\n#### D. Menu Integration\n```python\n# In menu_bar.py, add to View menu or new Tools menu\ntools_menu = self.addMenu(\"&Tools\")\ntools_menu.addAction(self._make_action(\n    \"Focus Mode\",\n    \"Ctrl+Shift+F\",\n    self._bus_publish_factory(\"focus.toggle_requested\")\n))\n```\n\n#### E. Module-Level Integration\n```python\n# Create flockdesk/modules/focus_mode/\n# OR integrate into existing modules via event-bus subscriptions\n\nclass FocusModeManager(QtCore.QObject):\n    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, settings_service: SettingsService):\n        self._bus = event_bus\n        self._settings = settings_service\n        \n        # Subscribe to toggle requests\n        self._bus.subscribe(\"focus.toggle_requested\", self._on_toggle_requested)\n        # Subscribe to settings changes\n        self._bus.subscribe(\"settings.updated\", self._on_settings_changed)\n    \n    def _on_toggle_requested(self, event: Event) -> None:\n        settings = self._settings.get(\"focus_mode\")\n        new_state = not settings[\"enabled\"]\n        self._settings.set_value(\"focus_mode\", \"enabled\", new_state)\n        \n        # Publish state change\n        self._bus.publish(\n            Event(\n                topic=\"settings\",\n                name=\"focus_mode.changed\",\n                payload=FocusModeChanged(enabled=new_state, ...)\n            )\n        )\n    \n    def _on_settings_changed(self, event: Event) -> None:\n        if event.payload.get(\"namespace\") == \"focus_mode\":\n            # Apply focus mode: hide modules, disable notifications, etc.\n            settings = self._settings.get(\"focus_mode\")\n            if settings[\"enabled\"]:\n                self._apply_focus_mode(settings)\n            else:\n                self._disable_focus_mode()\n```\n\n---\n\n### 9. **Key Architectural Patterns Summary**\n\n| Pattern | Where Used | Why | Implementation |\n|---------|-----------|-----|-----------------|\n| **Event-Driven** | EventBus, services | Decoupling, async support | Pub/sub with wildcard routing |\n| **MVVM** | All modules (chat, dashboard) | Separation of concerns, testability | QObject + Qt signals |\n| **Singleton** | EventBus, SettingsService, Registry | Global state management | Thread-safe lazy initialization |\n| **Registry** | CommandRegistry, EventRegistry | Plugin extensibility | Dynamic registration at runtime |\n| **Protocol/Interface** | Menu, commands, theme manager | Loose coupling | Python Protocol types |\n| **Context Manager** | SettingsService (TransientOverride) | Resource cleanup, testing | `__enter__/__exit__` |\n| **Broker** | EventBus | Multi-process safety | Separate OS process fan-out |\n| **Decorator** | `@event`, `@register_settings_schema` | Ergonomic registration | Type-safe registration at module load |\n\n---\n\n### 10. **Critical Implementation Notes for Focus Mode**\n\n1. **Thread Safety**: All EventBus operations are thread-safe; use them from any thread\n2. **Process Safety**: Events are pickled, so payloads must be pickleable\n3. **Async Integration**: SettingsService runs in background thread; don't block UI\n4. **Event Ordering**: No guaranteed ordering between processes; use correlation_ids for request-reply\n5. **Persistence**: SettingsService auto-persists to `~/.flockdesk/focus_mode.json` (appdirs)\n6. **Hot-Reload**: Commands can be re-registered; events can be added at runtime via decorator\n7. **Error Handling**: All event handlers wrapped in exception-safe slots; exceptions logged but don't crash\n8. **Memory**: Subscriptions must be explicit; unsubscribe to avoid leaks (returned `_Subscription` object)\n\n---\n\n**File Paths (All Absolute):**\n- EventBus: `/app/project/flockdesk/core/ipc/event_bus.py`\n- EventTypes: Type,\n    TypeVar,\n)\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"EventTopic\",\n    \"Event\",\n    \"EventRegistry\",\n    \"event\",\n    \"PayloadT\",\n]\n\n###############################################################################\n# Event Topic & Name Declarations\n###############################################################################\n\nclass EventTopic(str, Enum):\n    \"\"\"\n    Logical top-level buckets for events flowing through the domains\n    CHAT = \"chat\"\n    WHITEBOARD = \"whiteboard\"\n    PRESENCE = \"presence\"\n    FILES = \"files\"\n    SETTINGS = \"settings\"\n    PLUGIN = \"plugin\"\n\n    # System / infrastructure domains\n    SYSTEM = \"system\"\n    LOGGING = \"logging\"\n    METRICS = \"metrics\"\n\n    def __str__(self) -> str:  # pragma: no cover\n        return to multiple subscribers.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    topic: EventTopic\n    name: str\n    payload: PayloadT\n    timestamp: datetime = field(\n        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n    )\n    correlation_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: uuid.uuid4().hex)\n    reply_to: Optional[str] = None\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n    # \"\"\"\n        Serialize event to JSON for IPC (e.g. when crossing process boundary).\n        \"\"\"\n        as_dict = {\n            \"topic\": self.topic.value,\n            \"name\": self.name,\n            \"payload\": self.payload,\n            \"timestamp\": self.timestamp.isoformat(),\n            \"correlation_id\": self.correlation_id,\n            \"reply_to\": self.reply_to,\n        }\n        return json.dumps(as_dict, separators=(\",\", \":\"))\n\n\n###############################################################################\n# Registry Infrastructure\n###############################################################################\n\nclass _SingletonMeta(type):\n    \"\"\"Thread-safe singleton metaclass used for `EventRegistry`.\"\"\"\n\n    _instance: Optional[\"EventRegistry\"] = None\n    _lock: threading.Lock = threading.Lock()\n\n    def __call__(cls, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any):\n        if cls._instance is None:\n            with cls._lock:\n                if cls._instance is None:  # double-checked locking\n                    cls._instance = super().__call__(*args, **kwargs)\n        return cls._instance\n\n\nclass EventRegistry(metaclass=_SingletonMeta):\n    \"\"\"\n    Keep track of *all* event classes and their associated FQNs.\n\n    Plugins are allowed to add to this registry at runtime.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self) -> None:\n        # _events maps FQN -> payload dataclass (or a typing.Mapping subclass)\n        self._events: Dict[str, Type[Mapping[str, Any]]] = {}\n        self._lock = threading.RLock()\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #\n    # Public API\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #\n\n    def register(\n        self,\n        *,  # keyword-only for clarity\n        topic: EventTopic,\n        name: str,\n        payload_type: Type[Mapping[str, Any]],\n        override: bool = False,\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Register a new event definition.\n\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        topic:\n            Top-level domain of the event.\n        name:\n            Sub-name without topic prefix (e.g. 'message.sent').\n        payload_type:\n            A `dataclass` *or* any `Mapping[str, Any]` derived type describing\n            the contract of the event's payload.\n        override:\n            Whether an already registered FQN should be overwritten.\n        \"\"\"\n        if not name or \".\" not in name and topic not in (\n            EventTopic.SYSTEM,\n            EventTopic.LOGGING,\n            EventTopic.METRICS,\n        ):\n            raise ValueError(\n                \"Event name must contain at least one '.' to express hierarchy \"\n                f\"(got '{name}')\"\n            )\n\n        fqn = f\"{topic.value}.{name}\"\n\n        with self._lock:\n            if fqn in self._events and not override:\n                raise KeyError(\n                    f\"Event '{fqn}' already registered. \"\n                    \"Use 'override=True' to update the definition.\"\n                )\n            self._events[fqn] = payload_type\n\n    def payload_type_for(self, fqn: str) -> Type[Mapping[str, Any]]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Retrieve the expected payload type for a given event FQN.\n        \"\"\"\n        try:\n            return self._events[fqn]\n        except KeyError as exc:\n            raise KeyError(\n                f\"Event '{fqn}' not known to registry – did you forget to \"\n                \"import or register the corresponding plugin?\"\n            ) from exc\n\n    def is_known(self, fqn: str) -> bool:\n        return fqn in self._events\n\n    @property\n    def all_events(self) -> Mapping[str, Type[Mapping[str, Any]]]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Immutable view on currently registered event author_id: str\n    ...     content: str\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def decorator(payload_cls: Type[PayloadT]) -> Type[PayloadT]:\n        EventRegistry().register(\n            topic=topic,\n            name=name,\n            payload_type=payload_cls,\n            override=False,\n        )\n        return payload_cls\n\n    return decorator\n\n\n###############################################################################\n# Built-in Event Payloads\n###############################################################################\n\n# --- CHAT ------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n\n@dataclass(slots=True, frozen=True)\nclass _PayloadBase(Mapping[str, Any]):\n    \"\"\"\n    Minimal Mapping implementation so payloads can be treated\n    like dictionaries while still return (field.name for field in dataclasses.fields(self))\n\n    def __len__(self) -> int:  # noqa: D401\n        return len(dataclasses.fields(self))\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #\n    # Dict & JSON helpers\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #\n\n    def to_dict(self) ->           f\"Type mismatch for '{namespace}.{key}': \"\n                    f\"expected {expected_type}, got {type(value)}.\"\n                )\n\n            setattr(instance, key, value)\n invoked when FlockDesk shuts down.\n        \"\"\"\n        self._stop_event.set()\n        if self._save_thread and self._save_thread.is_alive():\n            self._save_thread.join()\n        self._flush_all()\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #\n    # Internal helpers\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #\n    def _mark_dirty(self, namespace: str) -> None:\n        self._dirty_namespaces.add(namespace)\n        if self._autosave is False:\n            # Persist synchronously when autosave is disabled.\n            self._flush(namespace)\n        else:\n            _logger.debug(\"Marked namespace '%s' as DIRTY.\", namespace)\n\n        # Publish change notification.\n        if self._event_bus:\n       payload = meta.to_dict(instance)\n            payload[\"__version__\"] = meta.version\n            self._files[namespace].write(payload)\n            self._dirty_namespaces.discard(namespace)\n       time.sleep(self._SAVE_INTERVAL_SEC)\n            if self._dirty_namespaces:\n                _logger.debug(\n                    \"Auto-saving %d namespaces …\", len(self._dirty_namespaces)\n                )\n            self._flush_all()\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# “Public” helper – decorator to register dataclass schemas more The implementation purposefully avoids external dependencies and\n    12→relies solely on the standard library (``asyncio``/``multiprocessing``) so that\n    13→plugins do not need to vendor additional wheels.\n    14→\n    15→Design goals\n    16→============\n    17→\n    18→1. The implementation purposefully avoids external dependencies and\nrelies solely on the standard library (``asyncio``/``multiprocessing``) so that\nplugins do not need to vendor additional wheels.\n\nDesign goals\n============\n\n1. Publish / subscribe with topic-based routing and Unix‐shell wild-card\n   matching (``fnmatch``) so that a handler can listen to groups\n   (e.g. ``\"presence.*\"``).\n\n2. Support both synchronous **and** asynchronous handlers without forcing plugin\n   authors to learn ``asyncio``.\n\n3. Provide a *request / reply* pattern with correlation-IDs and automatic\n   timeout handling—essential for client–server style interactions.\n\n4. Remain safe across processes.  A *broker* process (spawned lazily and managed\n   via :pymod:`multiprocessing.managers`) forwards events between producer and\n   consumer processes transparently.\n\n5. Offer graceful shutdown semantics so that the application can quit quickly\n   even under heavy traffic.\n\nThe broker architecture may feel heavyweight for local desktop IPC, but it\nguarantees that crashing micro-front-ends cannot bring down the other windows.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport contextlib\nimport fnmatch\nimport inspect\nimport logging\nimport queue\nimport threading\nimport time\nimport uuid\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom multiprocessing import Event as MpEvent\nfrom multiprocessing import Process, Queue\nfrom multiprocessing.managers import BaseManager\nfrom types import TracebackType\nfrom typing import (\n    Any,\n    Awaitable,\n    Callable,\n    Dict,\n    List,\n    Mapping,\n    MutableMapping,\n    Optional,\n    Tuple,\n    Type,\n    Union,\n)\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Logging\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n_log = logging.getLogger(\"flockdesk.ipc.event_bus\")\n_log.addHandler(logging.NullHandler())\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Exceptions\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\nclass EventBusError(RuntimeError):\n    \"\"\"Base-class for all event-bus exceptions.\"\"\"\n\n\nclass HandlerTimeout(EventBusError):\n    \"\"\"Raised by ``request`` when no reply arrived in time.\"\"\"\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Event primitives\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\n@dataclass(slots=True, frozen=True)\nclass Event:\n    \"\"\"\n    A single immutable message traveling through the *in-memory* event bus.\n\n    Attributes\n    ----------\n    topic:\n        Hierarchical, ``dot``-separated topic name\n        (e.g. ``\"presence.updated\"``).\n    payload:\n        Opaque data expected by subscribers.  Must be *pickleable* because the\n        broker relies on :pymod:`multiprocessing.Queue`.\n    headers:\n        Optional free-form metadata such as authentication tokens or tracing\n        information.  Header values must again be pickleable.\n    event_id:\n        Unique identifier for tracing/debugging purposes.\n    timestamp:\n        Epoch seconds (float) at creation time.\n    origin_pid:\n        The operating-system process identifier of the sender.  Helpful when the\n        same user runs multiple instances of FlockDesk on the same machine.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    topic: str\n    payload: Any\n    headers: Mapping[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    event_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: uuid.uuid4().hex, init=False)\n    timestamp: float = field(default_factory=time.time, init=False)\n    origin_pid: int = field(default_factory=lambda: threading.get_native_id(), init=False)\n\n    def __post_init__(self) -> None:  # noqa: D401\n        \"\"\"Validate that the topic is non-empty.\"\"\"\n        if not self.topic or not isinstance(self.topic, str):\n            raise ValueError(\"Event.topic must be a non-empty string\")\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Subscription Handle\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\nclass _Subscription:\n    \"\"\"\n    Lightweight handle that allows clients to stop listening without keeping\n    implementation details around.  Instances can also be used as *async*\n    context-managers.\n\n    NOTE: Users are **not** expected to instantiate this class directly.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    __slots__ = (\"_topic\", \"_handler\", \"_bus\", \"_active\")\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        topic: str,\n        handler: \"Handler\",\n        bus: \"EventBus\",\n    ) -> None:\n        self._topic = topic\n        self._handler = handler\n        self._bus = bus\n        self._active = True\n\n    async def __aenter__(self) -> \"_Subscription\":\n        return self\n\n    async def __aexit__(\n        self,\n        exc_type: Optional[Type[BaseException]],\n        exc: Optional[BaseException],\n        tb: Optional[TracebackType],\n    ) -> None:\n        self.unsubscribe()\n\n    def unsubscribe(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Remove the handler from the bus.\"\"\"\n        if self._active:\n            self._bus._remove_handler(self._topic, self._handler)  # noqa: SLF001\n            self._active = False\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Type aliases\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nSyncHandler = Callable[[Event], None]\nAsyncHandler = Callable[[Event], Awaitable[None]]\nHandler = Union[SyncHandler, AsyncHandler]\n\n# Internal maps\n_HandlerMap = Dict[str, List[Handler]]\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Broker (separate process)\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n# Queues are *multiprocessing* queues, therefore pickle based and safe between\n# forked/spawned processes.\n_InboundQueue = Queue  # alias for readability\n_OutboundQueue = Queue\n\n\nclass _Broker(Process):\n    \"\"\"\n    Spin-up a separate process that forwards a single *inbound* queue to many\n    *outbound* queues (fan-out).\n\n    Each process that calls ``EventBus.connect()`` will create its own outbound\n    queue, while the producing bus writes to a single inbound queue.\n\n    Diagram::\n\n        ┌────────────────────┐\n        │  client process A  │──┐\n        └────────────────────┘  │  ┌────────────┐\n                                ├─▶│            │\n        ┌────────────────────┐  │  │            │\n        │  client process B  │──┼─▶│   BROKER   │──▶ Inbound\n        └────────────────────┘  │  │            │    Queue\n                                └─▶│            │\n                                   └────────────┘\n\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, inbound: _InboundQueue, shutdown_evt: MpEvent):\n        super().__init__(name=\"FlockDeskEventBroker\", daemon=True)\n        self._inbound: _InboundQueue = inbound\n        self._shutdown_evt: MpEvent = shutdown_evt\n        self._outbounds: List[_OutboundQueue] = []\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    # Public API for other processes (via SyncManager)\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    def register_consumer(self) -> _OutboundQueue:\n        \"\"\"\n        Called by a client process to obtain a dedicated outbound queue.\n        \"\"\"\n        q: _OutboundQueue = Queue()\n        self._outbounds.append(q)\n        _log.debug(\"Broker registered new consumer queue (total=%d)\", len(self._outbounds))\n        return q\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    # Main loop\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    def run(self) -> None:  # noqa: D401\n        _log.info(\"Event-bus broker running (PID=%d)\", self.pid)\n        try:\n            while not self._shutdown_evt.is_set():\n                try:\n                    evt: Event = self._inbound.get(timeout=0.1)\n                except queue.Empty:\n                    continue\n\n                # Broadcast\n                for q in self._outbounds:\n                    try:\n                        q.put_nowait(evt)\n                    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n                        _log.exception(\"Failed to forward event to queue %s\", q)\n        finally:\n            _log.info(\"Event-bus broker shutting down (PID=%d)\", self.pid)\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Multiprocessing manager that exposes broker methods\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\nclass _BusManager(BaseManager):\n    pass\n\n\n# have to register after class definition\n_BusManager.register(\"register_consumer\", callable=None)  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# EventBus (public)\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\nclass EventBus:\n    \"\"\"\n    Public entry point used by every micro-front-end.\n\n    Processes talk to a shared broker, threads inside the same process share\n    exactly one :class:`EventBus` instance (thread-safe singleton).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    _singleton: \"EventBus\" | None = None\n    _singleton_lock = threading.Lock()\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    # Construction helpers\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    @classmethod\n    def connect(cls) -> \"EventBus\":\n        \"\"\"\n        Return a *singleton* instance of :class:`EventBus` for the current\n        process.  Lazily starts the broker **once** per workstation.\n        \"\"\"\n        with cls._singleton_lock:\n            if cls._singleton is None:\n                cls._singleton = cls._create()\n        return cls._singleton\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    # Instance-level lifecycle\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    @classmethod\n    def _create(cls) -> \"EventBus\":\n        # 1. Spawn or attach to broker\n        inbound: _InboundQueue = Queue()\n        shutdown_evt: MpEvent = MpEvent()\n\n        broker = _Broker(inbound, shutdown_evt)\n        broker.start()\n\n        # 2. Register via SyncManager so that we can obtain our *private* queue\n        manager = _BusManager(\n            address=(\"localhost\", 0), authkey=uuid.uuid4().hex.encode(\"ascii\")\n        )\n        # The manager itself runs in a sub-process—start it:\n        manager.start()\n        # Now we can register the broker method for remote access\n        manager.register(\"register_consumer\", broker.register_consumer)  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n\n        consumer_q: _OutboundQueue = manager.register_consumer()  # type: ignore[attr-defined]\n\n        return cls(\n            inbound=inbound,\n            outbound=consumer_q,\n            shutdown_evt=shutdown_evt,\n            broker=broker,\n            manager=manager,\n        )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    # dunder\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        *,\n        inbound: _InboundQueue,\n        outbound: _OutboundQueue,\n        shutdown_evt: MpEvent,\n        broker: _Broker,\n        manager: _BusManager,\n    ) -> None:\n        self._inbound = inbound\n        self._outbound = outbound\n        self._broker = broker\n        self._shutdown_evt = shutdown_evt\n        self._manager = manager\n\n        self._handlers: _HandlerMap = {}\n        self._loop = asyncio.get_event_loop_policy().get_event_loop()\n        self._dispatch_task: asyncio.Task[None] | None = None\n\n        # Protect _handlers as multiple event-loop threads could touch it\n        self._h_lock = asyncio.Lock()\n\n        # Synchronize start\n        self._ensure_dispatcher()\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    # Public API\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    def subscribe(self, topic: str, handler: Handler) -> _Subscription:\n        \"\"\"\n        Register *handler* for the given *topic* pattern.\n\n        Wildcards: Use ``'*'`` to match one segment, ``'**'`` to match\n        anything recursively (see :pymod:`fnmatch`).\n\n        Returns\n        -------\n        _Subscription\n            A tiny handle that can be used to *unsubscribe* again.\n        \"\"\"\n        if not callable(handler):\n            raise TypeError(\"Handler must be callable\")\n\n        _log.debug(\"Subscribing %s to topic=%s\", handler, topic)\n\n        # Keep sync handler as is, wrap non-coroutine functions so that we can\n        # `await` them uniformly.\n        if not inspect.iscoroutinefunction(handler):\n\n            async def _async_adapter(evt: Event, _h=handler) -> None:\n                await self._loop.run_in_executor(None, _h, evt)\n\n            adapted: Handler = _async_adapter\n        else:\n            adapted = handler  # type: ignore[assignment]\n\n        # Insert\n        async def _insert() -> None:\n            async with self._h_lock:\n                self._handlers.setdefault(topic, []).append(adapted)\n\n        asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(_insert(), self._loop).result()\n        return _Subscription(topic, adapted, self)\n\n    def publish(self, topic: str, payload: Any, headers: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Fire-and-forget publish.  Safe to call from **any** thread—will enqueue\n        the event into the broker’s *inbound* queue.\n        \"\"\"\n        evt = Event(topic=topic, payload=payload, headers=headers or {})\n        try:\n            self._inbound.put_nowait(evt)\n        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001\n            _log.exception(\"Failed to publish event %s: %s\", evt, exc)\n\n    async def request(\n        self,\n        topic: str,\n        payload: Any,\n        *,\n        timeout: float = 5.0,\n        headers: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None,\n    ) -> Event:\n        \"\"\"\n        Send a *request* and await the first reply matching the\n        ``correlation_id`` inside *timeout* seconds.\n\n        The server side should look at the ``reply_to`` header and publish the\n        answer to that topic including the ``correlation_id``.\n        \"\"\"\n        correlation_id = uuid.uuid4().hex\n        reply_topic = f\"__reply__.{correlation_id}\"\n\n        fut: asyncio.Future[Event] = self._loop.create_future()\n\n        # Local handler just completes the future\n        async def _reply_handler(evt: Event) -> None:\n            fut.set_result(evt)\n\n        subscription = self.subscribe(reply_topic, _reply_handler)\n\n        # Publish *request*\n        hdrs: Dict[str, Any] = dict(headers or {})\n        hdrs[\"reply_to\"] = reply_topic\n        hdrs[\"correlation_id\"] = correlation_id\n        self.publish(topic, payload, hdrs)\n\n        try:\n            return await asyncio.wait_for(fut, timeout=timeout)\n        except asyncio.TimeoutError as exc:\n            raise HandlerTimeout(f\"Request timed out after {timeout}s\") from exc\n        finally:\n            subscription.unsubscribe()\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    # Shutdown\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    def close(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Close the bus, stop the background tasks and terminate the broker.\n\n        **Important:** This method should be called exactly *once* at\n        application shutdown.\n        \"\"\"\n        _log.info(\"Shutting down EventBus in process=%d\", threading.get_native_id())\n\n        self._shutdown_evt.set()\n\n        if self._dispatch_task:\n            self._dispatch_task.cancel()\n\n        # Give the broker a moment to finish pending broadcasts\n        if self._broker.is_alive():\n            self._broker.join(timeout=1.0)\n        self._manager.shutdown()\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    # Internal helpers\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    def _remove_handler(self, topic: str, handler: Handler) -> None:\n        async def _remove() -> None:\n            async with self._h_lock:\n                lst = self._handlers.get(topic, [])\n                if handler in lst:\n                    lst.remove(handler)\n\n        asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(_remove(), self._loop).result()\n\n    def _ensure_dispatcher(self) -> None:\n        if self._dispatch_task is None or self._dispatch_task.done():\n            self._dispatch_task = self._loop.create_task(self._dispatcher())\n\n    async def _dispatcher(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Background coroutine that consumes events from the *outbound* queue and\n        delivers them to pattern-matched handlers.\n        \"\"\"\n        _log.debug(\"EventBus dispatcher started (thread=%s)\", threading.current_thread().name)\n        while not self._shutdown_evt.is_set():\n            try:\n                # Non-blocking read in *executor* because Queue.get is blocking but\n                # not async aware.\n                evt: Event = await self._loop.run_in_executor(None, self._outbound.get, True, 0.1)\n            except queue.Empty:\n                continue\n            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n                _log.exception(\"Unhandled exception while reading outbound queue\")\n                continue\n\n            await self._dispatch(evt)\n\n    async def _dispatch(self, evt: Event) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Match patterns and execute handlers concurrently.\"\"\"\n        async with self._h_lock:\n            # Flatten into (topic_pattern, handlers[])\n            items: List[Tuple[str, List[Handler]]] = list(self._handlers.items())\n\n        coros: List[Awaitable[None]] = []\n\n        for pattern, handlers in items:\n            if fnmatch.fnmatchcase(evt.topic, pattern):\n                coros.extend(h(evt) for h in handlers)\n\n        if not coros:\n            return  # no listeners\n\n        await asyncio.gather(*coros, return_exceptions=False)\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    # Context manager sugar\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    def __enter__(self) -> \"EventBus\":\n        return self\n\n    def __exit__(\n        self,\n        exc_type: Optional[Type[BaseException]],\n        exc: Optional[BaseException],\n        tb: Optional[TracebackType],\n    ) -> None:\n        self.close()\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Convenience helpers for library-wide singleton access\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n_bus: EventBus | None = None\n_bus_lock = threading.Lock()\n\n\ndef get_event_bus() -> EventBus:\n    \"\"\"\n    Return a process-global :class:`EventBus` instance, creating it on first use.\n    \"\"\"\n    global _bus\n    with _bus_lock:\n        if _bus is None:\n            _bus = EventBus.connect()\n        return _bus\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Example usage (guarded)\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":  # pragma: no cover\n    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.DEBUG)\n\n    async def main() -> None:\n        bus = get_event_bus()\n\n        # Example async handler\n        async def on_presence(evt: Event) -> None:\n            print(\"[async] received\", evt.topic, evt.payload)\n\n        # Example sync handler\n        def on_any(evt: Event) -> None:\n            print(\"[sync ] saw\", evt.topic, evt.payload)\n\n        bus.subscribe(\"presence.*\", on_presence)\n        bus.subscribe(\"**\", on_any)\n\n        # Publish from different thread\n        def _publisher() -> None:\n            b = get_event_bus()\n            b.publish(\"presence.updated\", {\"user\": \"alice\", \"status\": \"online\"})\n\n        threading.Thread(target=_publisher, daemon=True).start()\n\n        await asyncio.sleep(1.0)\n        bus.close()\n\n    asyncio.run(main())\n```","numLines":588,"startLine":1,"totalLines":588}}}

3s window\n\n    def _emit_typing_end(self, user_id: str) -> None:\n        self.typingStatusChanged.emit(user_id, False)\n        self._typing_users.pop(user_id, None)\n\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    def _on_remote_update(self, event: Event) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Handle remote edits or deletions.\"\"\"\n        payload = event.payload\n        if payload[\"channel_id\"] != self._channel_id:\n            return\n\n        with self._lock:\n            for idx, m in enumerate(self._messages):\n                if m.msg_id == payload[\"msg_id\"]:\n                    updated = ChatMessage(\n                        msg_id=m.msg_id,\n                        channel_id=m.channel_id,\n                        sender_id=m.sender_id,\n                        sender_display=m.sender_display,\n                        content=payload.get(\"content\", m.content),\n                        timestamp=m.timestamp,\n                        edited=True,\n                        attachments=[Path(p) for p in payload.get(\"attachments\", [])],\n                    )\n                    self._messages[idx] = updated\n                    self.messageReplaced.emit(updated)\n                    _log.debug(\"Message %s updated from remote\", m.msg_id)\n                    break\n\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    # Draft persistence\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    def _restore_draft_async(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Restore draft in a background thread to keep UI responsive.\"\"\"\n\n        def _worker(vm: \"ChatViewModel\") -> None:\n            if vm._draft_path.exists():\n                try:\n                    data = json.loads(vm._draft_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n                    draft = data.get(\"draft\", \"\")\n                    _log.debug(\"Draft restored for channel %s\", vm._channel_id)\n                    # The plugin\n        receives ownership of its widget but the life-cycle is still managed\n        by ChatWidget.\n\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        widget:\n            The Qt widget that represents the plugin’s UI surface.\n        plugin_id:\n            Unique identifier used to track and remove the plugin later.\n        \"\"\"\n        widget.setProperty(\"plugin_id\", plugin_id)\n        self._plugin_container.addWidget(widget)\n        self._plugin_container.setCurrentWidget(widget)\n        self._plugin_container.setVisible(True)\n\n        _LOGGER.info(\"Plugin %s attached to ChatWidget\", plugin_id)\n\n    @_t.final\n    def detach_plugin(self, plugin_id: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Removes a previously attached plugin from the view.\"\"\"\n        for i in range(self._plugin_container.count()):\n            page = self._plugin_container.widget(i)\n            if page.property(\"plugin_id\") == plugin_id:\n                self._plugin_container.removeWidget(page)\n                page.deleteLater()\n                break\n\n        visible = self._plugin_container.count() > 0\n        self._plugin_container.setVisible(visible)\n\n        _LOGGER.info(\"Plugin %s detached from ChatWidget\", plugin_id)\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #\n    # Qt life-cycle\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #\n    def closeEvent(self, event: QtGui.QCloseEvent) -> None:  # noqa: N802\n        \"\"\"Clean up Bus subscriptions to avoid dangling callbacks.\"\"\"\n        self._bus.unsubscribe_owner(self)\n        super().closeEvent(event)\n```","numLines":315,"startLine":1,"totalLines":315}}}
