Each card is a plugin that exposes a QWidget\nand registers itself to the internal event-bus.  Users can re-arrange,\nremove, and add cards simply by dragging & dropping new plugins from the\nplugin-palette or by right-clicking a card and selecting *Remove*.\n\nDesign goals\n------------\n1. MVVM separation – the widget delegates all non-UI work to the\n   DashboardViewModel.\n2. Event driven – real-time updates via the global EventBus.\n3. Plugin friendly – cards are loaded/unloaded via the PluginManager.\n4. Robustness – every plugin is sandboxed; a faulty widget is replaced\n   with a red *crash card* rather than crashing the entire dashboard.\n\nThe implementation purposefully stays clear of concrete business-logic.\nProjects embedding this component must provide:\n\n* flockdesk.core.event_bus.EventBus              (pub/sub)\n* flockdesk.core.plugins.manager.PluginManager   (plugin life-cycle)\n* flockdesk.core.settings.AppSettings            (persistent settings)\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport logging\nimport traceback\nfrom contextlib import suppress\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Dict, Optional, Type\n\nfrom PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QEvent, QObject, QMimeData, QPoint, Signal, Slot, QSize\nfrom PySide6.QtGui import QAction, QContextMenuEvent, QDragEnterEvent, QDropEvent, QIcon\nfrom PySide6.QtWidgets import (\n    QApplication,\n    QFrame,\n    QGridLayout,\n    QLabel,\n    QMenu,\n    QMessageBox,\n    QSizePolicy,\n    QVBoxLayout,\n    QWidget,\n)\n\n# External project modules (imported lazily to keep this file compile-time safe)\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef _lazy_import(path: str):\n    \"\"\"\n    Lazy import helper so that unit-tests can stub out heavy deps like Qt.\n    \"\"\"\n    import importlib\n\n    module, _, sym = path.rpartition(\".\")\n    mod = importlib.import_module(module)\n    return getattr(mod, sym)\n\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Models\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass CardMeta:\n    \"\"\"\n    Meta information stored for every card inside the dashboard grid.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    plugin_id: str\n    plugin_name: str\n    widget: QWidget\n    col_span: int = 1\n    row_span: int = 1\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# View-Model\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\nclass DashboardViewModel(QObject):\n    \"\"\"\n    The *VM* coordinating event-bus messages, plugin life-cycle and layout\n    persistence.  The view (DashboardWidget) only performs tasks strictly\n    related to UI rendering.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Signals exposed to the view\n    themeChanged = Signal(str)  # dark / light / custom-name\n    settingsChanged = Signal()\n    cardCrashed = Signal(str)  # plugin_id\n\n    # Internal MIME-type used to drag plugins from palette to the dashboard\n    MIME_TYPE = \"application/x-flockdesk-plugin-id\"\n\n    def __init__(self, parent: Optional[QObject] = None) -> None:\n        super().__init__(parent)\n        self._event_bus: \"EventBus\" = _lazy_import(\n            \"flockdesk.core.event_bus.EventBus\"\n        ).instance()\n        self._plugin_manager: \"PluginManager\" = _lazy_import(\n            \"flockdesk.core.plugins.manager.PluginManager\"\n        ).instance()\n        self._settings: \"AppSettings\" = _lazy_import(\n            \"flockdesk.core.settings.AppSettings\"\n        ).instance()\n\n        # plugin_id -> CardMeta\n        self._cards: Dict[str, CardMeta] = {}\n\n        self._subscribe_events()\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- API\n\n    def load_initial_cards(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Load cards remembered from the previous session, honoring the user’s\n        persistent layout.\n        \"\"\"\n        stored_cards = self._settings.dashboard.cards\n        logger.debug(\"Loading persisted dashboard cards: %s\", stored_cards)\n        for plugin_id in stored_cards:\n            self.add_card(plugin_id)\n\n    def add_card(self, plugin_id: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Instantiate a plugin widget and register it as a CardMeta.\"\"\"\n        if plugin_id in self._cards:\n            logger.info(\"Card for plugin '%s' already present – skipping\", plugin_id)\n            return\n        try:\n            plugin_cls: Type[\"BasePlugin\"] = self._plugin_manager.get_plugin(plugin_id)\n            plugin: \"BasePlugin\" = plugin_cls()\n            widget = plugin.build_widget()  # type: ignore[attr-defined]\n        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001\n            logger.error(\"Could not instantiate plugin '%s': %s\", plugin_id, exc)\n            self.cardCrashed.emit(plugin_id)\n            return\n\n        meta = CardMeta(\n            plugin_id=plugin_id,\n            plugin_name=plugin.meta.name,  # type: ignore[attr-defined]\n            widget=widget,\n        )\n        self._cards[plugin_id] = meta\n        self._settings.dashboard.cards.append(plugin_id)\n        self._settings.save()\n\n        # Must be emitted so the view can insert the widget in the layout.\n        self.settingsChanged.emit()\n\n    def remove_card(self, plugin_id: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Remove plugin card from internal model and persist change.\"\"\"\n        if plugin_id not in self._cards:\n            return\n\n        meta = self._cards.pop(plugin_id)\n        meta.widget.deleteLater()\n\n        with suppress(ValueError):\n            self._settings.dashboard.cards.remove(plugin_id)\n        self._settings.save()\n        self.settingsChanged.emit()\n\n    def cards(self) -> Dict[str, CardMeta]:\n        \"\"\"Return the currently active cards.\"\"\"\n        return self._cards\n\n    # ----------------------------------------------------------------- Events\n\n    def _subscribe_events(self) -> None:\n        # Theme changes\n        self._event_bus.subscribe(\"ui.theme.changed\", self._on_theme_changed)\n        # Global settings updated\n        self._event_bus.subscribe(\"settings.updated\", self._on_settings_updated)\n\n    async def _on_theme_changed(self, event: \"Event\") -> None:  # noqa: D401\n        theme_name = event.payload.get(\"theme\")\n        logger.debug(\"Theme change event received: %s\", theme_name)\n        self.themeChanged.emit(theme_name)\n\n    async def _on_settings_updated(self, event: \"Event\") -> None:  # noqa: D401\n        logger.debug(\"Settings updated event received\")\n        self.settingsChanged.emit()\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# View\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\nclass CrashCard(QFrame):\n    \"\"\"\n    Fallback widget shown when a plugin fails to initialise or crashes\n    during runtime.  It keeps the dashboard operational while making it\n    obvious which plugin misbehaved.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, plugin_id: str, parent: Optional[QWidget] = None) -> None:\n        super().__init__(parent)\n        self.setObjectName(\"crashCard\")\n        self.setFrameStyle(QFrame.Panel | QFrame.Raised)\n        self.setLineWidth(2)\n\n        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)\n        label = QLabel(\n            f\"⚠️ Plugin '{plugin_id}' failed to load.\\n\"\n            \"Right-click to remove the card.\"\n        )\n        label.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)\n        label.setWordWrap(True)\n        layout.addWidget(label)\n\n        self.setStyleSheet(\n            \"\"\"\n            #crashCard {\n                background: #b00020;\n                color: white;\n                border-radius: 6px;\n            }\n            \"\"\"\n        )\n\n\nclass DashboardWidget(QWidget):\n    \"\"\"\n    The UI surface showing multiple plugin cards in a responsive grid.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    GRID_COLUMNS = 4  # TODO: make configurable\n\n    def __init__(self, view_model: Optional[DashboardViewModel] = None) -> None:\n        super().__init__()\n\n        # If not injected (unit-tests), create a default one\n        self._vm = view_model or DashboardViewModel()\n\n        self.setAcceptDrops(True)\n        self.setObjectName(\"flockdeskDashboard\")\n        self._build_ui()\n\n        # Connect signals\n        self._vm.themeChanged.connect(self._apply_theme)\n        self._vm.settingsChanged.connect(self._rebuild_cards)\n        self._vm.cardCrashed.connect(self._handle_card_crash)\n\n        # Initial population\n        self._vm.load_initial_cards()\n        self._rebuild_cards()\n\n    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- UI init\n\n    def _build_ui(self) -> None:\n        self._grid = QGridLayout()\n        self._grid.setSpacing(12)\n        self._grid.setContentsMargins(12, 12, 12, 12)\n        self.setLayout(self._grid)\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------ Card helpers\n\n    def _handle_card_crash(self, plugin_id: str) -> None:\n        logger.debug(\"Handling crash of plugin '%s'\", plugin_id)\n        crash_card = CrashCard(plugin_id)\n        self._insert_widget(crash_card)\n\n    def _rebuild_cards(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Re-create the grid whenever cards were added/removed or when a plugin\n        crashed.\n        \"\"\"\n        while self._grid.count():\n            item = self._grid.takeAt(0)\n            if widget := item.widget():\n                widget.setParent(None)\n                widget.deleteLater()\n\n        col, row = 0, 0\n        for meta in self._vm.cards().values():\n            self._insert_widget(meta.widget, row=row, col=col)\n            col += 1\n            if col >= self.GRID_COLUMNS:\n                col = 0\n                row += 1\n\n        self._grid.setRowStretch(row + 1, 1)\n        self._grid.setColumnStretch(self.GRID_COLUMNS, 1)\n        self.updateGeometry()\n\n    def _insert_widget(self, widget: QWidget, *, row: int | None = None, col: int | None = None) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Insert `widget` into the next free cell unless `row/col` is provided.\n        \"\"\"\n        if row is None or col is None:\n            position = self._next_free_cell()\n            row, col = position\n\n        widget.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Expanding)\n        self._grid.addWidget(widget, row, col)\n        logger.debug(\"Inserted widget at row=%s col=%s\", row, col)\n\n    def _next_free_cell(self) -> tuple[int, int]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Calculate the next free slot in the grid using a simple left-to-right,\n        top-to-bottom algorithm.\n        \"\"\"\n        count = self._grid.count()\n        col = count % self.GRID_COLUMNS\n        row = count // self.GRID_COLUMNS\n        return row, col\n\n    # ----------------------------------------------------------- Drag & drop\n\n    def dragEnterEvent(self, event: QDragEnterEvent) -> None:\n        mime: QMimeData = event.mimeData()\n        if mime.hasFormat(DashboardViewModel.MIME_TYPE):\n            event.setDropAction(Qt.CopyAction)\n            event.acceptProposedAction()\n        else:\n            super().dragEnterEvent(event)\n\n    def dropEvent(self, event: QDropEvent) -> None:\n        mime: QMimeData = event.mimeData()\n        if not mime.hasFormat(DashboardViewModel.MIME_TYPE):\n            super().dropEvent(event)\n            return\n\n        plugin_id_bytes = mime.data(DashboardViewModel.MIME_TYPE)\n        plugin_id = bytes(plugin_id_bytes).decode()\n\n        logger.debug(\"Plugin dropped on dashboard: %s\", plugin_id)\n        self._vm.add_card(plugin_id)\n        event.acceptProposedAction()\n\n    # ---------------------------------------------------------- Context menu\n\n    def contextMenuEvent(self, event: QContextMenuEvent) -> None:\n        pos = event.globalPos()\n        widget_at_pos = self.childAt(event.pos())\n\n        menu = QMenu(self)\n        if isinstance(widget_at_pos, CrashCard):\n            plugin_id = self._plugin_id_for_widget(widget_at_pos)\n            if plugin_id:\n                remove_action = QAction(\"Remove card\", self)\n                remove_action.triggered.connect(\n                    lambda _=False, pid=plugin_id: self._vm.remove_card(pid)\n                )\n                menu.addAction(remove_action)\n        elif plugin_id := self._plugin_id_for_widget(widget_at_pos):\n            remove_action = QAction(\"Remove card\", self)\n            remove_action.triggered.connect(\n                lambda _=False, pid=plugin_id: self._vm.remove_card(pid)\n            )\n            settings_action = QAction(\"Card settings…\", self)\n            settings_action.triggered.connect(\n                lambda _=False, pid=plugin_id: self._open_plugin_settings(pid)\n            )\n            menu.addActions([settings_action, remove_action])\n        else:\n            refresh_action = QAction(\"Refresh dashboard\", self)\n            refresh_action.triggered.connect(self._rebuild_cards)\n            menu.addAction(refresh_action)\n\n        menu.exec(pos)\n\n    def _plugin_id_for_widget(self, child: Optional[QWidget]) -> Optional[str]:\n        if child is None:\n            return None\n        for pid, meta in self._vm.cards().items():\n            if meta.widget is child or child.isAncestorOf(meta.widget):\n                return pid\n        return None\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------- Helpers\n\n    def _open_plugin_settings(self, plugin_id: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Call the plugin’s settings UI or show fallback dialog.\"\"\"\n        plugin = self._vm._plugin_manager.get_plugin(plugin_id)  # pylint: disable=protected-access\n        if hasattr(plugin, \"open_settings\"):  # type: ignore[attr-defined]\n            plugin.open_settings(self)  # type: ignore[attr-defined]\n        else:\n            QMessageBox.information(\n                self,\n                \"Settings\",\n                f\"Plugin '{plugin_id}' does not expose a settings panel.\",\n            )\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------- Theming\n\n    @Slot(str)\n    def _apply_theme(self, theme_name: str) -> None:  # noqa: D401\n        \"\"\"\n        Apply QSS from the theme directory.  Blocks the UI minimally thanks to\n        async file I/O.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        async def _load_qss(name: str) -> str:  # noqa: D401\n            theme_dir = Path(QApplication.instance().property(\"themePath\"))  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n            qss_file = theme_dir / f\"{name}.qss\"\n            try:\n                return qss_file.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n            except FileNotFoundError:\n                logger.warning(\"Theme file missing: %s\", qss_file)\n                return \"\"\n\n        async def _apply() -> None:  # noqa: D401\n            qss = await _load_qss(theme_name)\n            QApplication.instance().setStyleSheet(qss)\n\n        asyncio.create_task(_apply(), name=\"dashboard.apply_theme\")\n\n    # ---------------------------------------------------------- Event filter\n\n    def event(self, e: QEvent):  # noqa: D401, N802\n        \"\"\"\n        Override event() to intercept plugin widget crashes.  Qt does not\n        natively raise Python exceptions that occur during event processing\n        inside child widgets.  By installing a global *except-hook* we can at\n        least replace the faulty widget with a CrashCard.\n        \"\"\"\n        try:\n            return super().event(e)\n        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n            traceback.print_exc()\n            crashed_widget = self.childAt(self.mapFromGlobal(QCursor.pos()))\n            plugin_id = self._plugin_id_for_widget(crashed_widget)\n            if plugin_id:\n                self._handle_card_crash(plugin_id)\n            return True  # Event handled\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Stand-alone test shim\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    import sys\n\n    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.DEBUG)\n\n    app = QApplication(sys.argv)\n    dash = DashboardWidget()\n    dash.resize(QSize(900, 600))\n    dash.show()\n\n    sys.exit(app.exec())\n```","numLines":458,"startLine":1,"totalLines":458}}}

declaration of event topics, event names, and helper utilities\nfor the in-process / cross-process event-bus powering FlockDesk’s desktop\nmicro-front-ends.\n\nThe goal of this module is to provide one single source of truth regarding\nwhat events exist, what their *fully–qualified names* (FQN) are, and which\nPython object is expected as the payload.\n\nWhy does that matter?\n---------------------\n1. **Type-Safety / Static Analysis** – Editors & CI pipelines can leverage\n   this module for auto-completion and mypy/pyright validation.\n2. **Documentation** – Event definitions live close to code instead of\n   being scattered across wikis.\n3. **Plug-in Friendliness** – 3rd-party extensions can *register* their own\n   events at runtime without monkey-patching anything.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport dataclasses\nimport json\nimport threading\nimport uuid\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom enum import Enum\nfrom types import MappingProxyType\nfrom typing import (\n    Any,\n    Callable,\n    Dict,\n    Mapping,\n    MutableMapping,\n    Optional,\n    Type,\n    TypeVar,\n)\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"EventTopic\",\n    \"Event\",\n    \"EventRegistry\",\n    \"event\",\n    \"PayloadT\",\n]\n\n###############################################################################\n# Event Topic & Name Declarations\n###############################################################################\n\nclass EventTopic(str, Enum):\n    \"\"\"\n    Logical top-level buckets for events flowing through the FlockDesk bus.\n\n    Topics loosely correspond to micro-front-ends or backend domains and help\n    routing as well as access-control decisions.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # User-facing / collaborative domains\n    CHAT = \"chat\"\n    WHITEBOARD = \"whiteboard\"\n    PRESENCE = \"presence\"\n    FILES = \"files\"\n    SETTINGS = \"settings\"\n    PLUGIN = \"plugin\"\n\n    # System / infrastructure domains\n    SYSTEM = \"system\"\n    LOGGING = \"logging\"\n    METRICS = \"metrics\"\n\n    def __str__(self) -> str:  # pragma: no cover\n        return self.value\n\n\n###############################################################################\n# Base Event Dataclass\n###############################################################################\n\nPayloadT = TypeVar(\"PayloadT\", bound=Mapping[str, Any])\n\n\n@dataclass(slots=True, frozen=True)\nclass Event:\n    \"\"\"\n    Runtime instance of an Event exchanged on the bus.\n\n    Immutable on purpose – mutability would open the door for tricky bugs\n    once the same event instance is fanned-out to multiple subscribers.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    topic: EventTopic\n    name: str\n    payload: PayloadT\n    timestamp: datetime = field(\n        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n    )\n    correlation_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: uuid.uuid4().hex)\n    reply_to: Optional[str] = None\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n    # Convenience helpers\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n    @property\n    def fqn(self) -> str:\n        \"\"\"\n        Fully-qualified event name (Topic + sub-name).\n\n        Example:\n        >>> evt.fqn\n        'chat.message.sent'\n        \"\"\"\n        return f\"{self.topic.value}.{self.name}\"\n\n    def to_json(self) -> str:\n        \"\"\"\n        Serialize event to JSON for IPC (e.g. when crossing process boundary).\n        \"\"\"\n        as_dict = {\n            \"topic\": self.topic.value,\n            \"name\": self.name,\n            \"payload\": self.payload,\n            \"timestamp\": self.timestamp.isoformat(),\n            \"correlation_id\": self.correlation_id,\n            \"reply_to\": self.reply_to,\n        }\n        return json.dumps(as_dict, separators=(\",\", \":\"))\n\n\n###############################################################################\n# Registry Infrastructure\n###############################################################################\n\nclass _SingletonMeta(type):\n    \"\"\"Thread-safe singleton metaclass used for `EventRegistry`.\"\"\"\n\n    _instance: Optional[\"EventRegistry\"] = None\n    _lock: threading.Lock = threading.Lock()\n\n    def __call__(cls, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any):\n        if cls._instance is None:\n            with cls._lock:\n                if cls._instance is None:  # double-checked locking\n                    cls._instance = super().__call__(*args, **kwargs)\n        return cls._instance\n\n\nclass EventRegistry(metaclass=_SingletonMeta):\n    \"\"\"\n    Keep track of *all* event classes and their associated FQNs.\n\n    Plugins are allowed to add to this registry at runtime.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self) -> None:\n        # _events maps FQN -> payload dataclass (or a typing.Mapping subclass)\n        self._events: Dict[str, Type[Mapping[str, Any]]] = {}\n        self._lock = threading.RLock()\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #\n    # Public API\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #\n\n    def register(\n        self,\n        *,  # keyword-only for clarity\n        topic: EventTopic,\n        name: str,\n        payload_type: Type[Mapping[str, Any]],\n        override: bool = False,\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Register a new event definition.\n\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        topic:\n            Top-level domain of the event.\n        name:\n            Sub-name without topic prefix (e.g. 'message.sent').\n        payload_type:\n            A `dataclass` *or* any `Mapping[str, Any]` derived type describing\n            the contract of the event's payload.\n        override:\n            Whether an already registered FQN should be overwritten.\n        \"\"\"\n EventBus Architecture\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/core/ipc/event_bus.py\n// Lines: 276-408\n\nclass EventBus:\n    \"\"\"\n    Public entry point used by every micro-front-end.\n\n    Processes talk to a shared broker, threads inside the same process share\n    exactly one :class:`EventBus` instance (thread-safe singleton).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def subscribe(self, topic: str, handler: Handler) -> _Subscription:\n        \"\"\"\n        Register *handler* for the given *topic* pattern.\n\n        Wildcards: Use ``'*'`` to match one segment, ``'**'`` to match\n        anything recursively (see :pymod:`fnmatch`).\n        \"\"\"\n        # ... implementation\n\n    def publish(self, topic: str, payload: Any, headers: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Fire-and-forget publish.  Safe to call from **any** thread—will enqueue\n        the event into the broker's *inbound* queue.\n        \"\"\"\n        evt = Event(topic=topic, payload=payload, headers=headers or {})\n        self._inbound.put_nowait(evt)\n```\n\n### 2. Event Types Pattern\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/core/ipc/event_types.py\n// Lines: 351-362\n\n@event(topic=EventTopic.SETTINGS, name=\"changed\")\n@dataclass(slots=True, frozen=True)\nclass SettingsChanged(_PayloadBase):\n    user_id: str\n    key: str\n    old_value: Any\n    new_value: Any\n    changed_at: datetime = field(\n        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n    )\n```\n\n### 3. SettingsService Pattern\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/core/services/settings_service.py\n// Lines: 231-258\n\ndef set_value(self, namespace: str, key: str, value: Any) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Update a single field in the namespace and enqueue the change for\n    persistence and event-bus broadcast.\n    \"\"\"\n    with self._lock:\n        meta = self._schemas.get(namespace)\n        instance = self._instances.get(namespace)\n        # ... validation ...\n        setattr(instance, key, value)\n        self._mark_dirty(namespace)\n```\n\n### 4. Commands System Pattern\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/core/shortcuts/commands.py\n// Lines: 221-262\n\ndef register(\n    self,\n    *,\n    id: str,\n    description: str,\n    sequence: str | QKeySequence,\n    handler: Callable[[], None],\n    enabled: Callable[[], bool] = lambda: True,\n    owner: Optional[str] = None,\n) -> ShortcutCommand:\n    \"\"\"\n    Registers a new command or raises :class:`DuplicateCommandError`.\n    \"\"\"\n```\n\n### 5. Menu Bar Pattern\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/core/shell/menu_bar.py\n// Lines: 231-234\n\n# View --------------------------------------------------------------\nview_menu = self.addMenu(\"&View\")\ntheme_menu = view_menu.addMenu(\"Theme\")\nself._theme_menu = theme_menu  # used later for dynamic population\n```\n\n### 6. Chat ViewModel MVVM Pattern\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/modules/chat/viewmodel/chat_vm.py\n// Lines: 77-116\n\nclass ChatViewModel(QtCore.QObject):\n    \"\"\"\n    Acts as the View-Model for a single chat channel.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # -------- Qt Signals ----------------------------------------------------#\n    messageAdded = QtCore.Signal(ChatMessage)  # new message appended\n    messageReplaced = QtCore.Signal(ChatMessage)  # edited or updated\n    typingStatusChanged = QtCore.Signal(str, bool)  # user_id, is_typing\n    errorOccurred = QtCore.Signal(str)  # human-readable error string\n    historyLoaded = QtCore.Signal(list)  # list[ChatMessage]\n\n    def __init__(self, channel_id: str, event_bus: EventBus, parent=None) -> None:\n        super().__init__(parent)\n        self._channel_id: str = channel_id\n        self._event_bus: EventBus = event_bus\n        # ...\n        self._register_event_subscriptions()\n```\n\n### 7. Dashboard ViewModel MVVM Pattern\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/modules/dashboard/viewmodel/dashboard_vm.py\n// Lines: 97-145\n\nclass DashboardViewModel(QObject):\n    \"\"\"\n    Exposes reactive Qt signals + imperative command API for the dashboard.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # ---------------------------- Qt-Signals --------------------------------#\n    widgetsChanged = Signal(list)          # List[DashboardWidget]\n    busyChanged = Signal(bool)             # bool\n    themeChanged = Signal(str)             # str\n    errorOccurred = Signal(str)            # str\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        *,\n        event_bus: EventBus,\n        plugin_manager: PluginManager,\n        settings: Settings,\n        # ...\n    ) -> None:\n        super().__init__(parent=parent)\n        self._event_bus = event_bus\n        # ...\n        self._register_event_listeners()\n```\n\n## Analysis\n\n### Architectural Patterns Identified\n\n1. **Event-Driven Architecture**: The application uses a central `EventBus` for decoupled communication between modules. The bus supports:\n   - Topic-based routing with wildcard matching (`fnmatch`)\n   - Both sync and async handlers\n   - Process-safe communication via a broker process\n   - Request/reply pattern with correlation IDs\n\n2. **MVVM Pattern**: Each module follows MVVM:\n   - **Model**: Domain data classes (e.g., `ChatMessage`, `DashboardWidget`)\n   - **ViewModel**: Qt QObject with signals for UI binding (e.g., `ChatViewModel`, `DashboardViewModel`)\n   - **View**: Qt widgets that connect to ViewModel signals (e.g., `ChatWidget`, `DashboardWidget`)\n\n3. **Service Layer**: Core services like `SettingsService` handle cross-cutting concerns:\n   - Namespace-based settings with dataclass schemas\n   - Automatic persistence with atomic JSON writes\n   - Event-bus broadcast on changes\n\n4. **Command Pattern**: The `ShortcutCommandRegistry` encapsulates actions as first-class objects:\n   - Commands are registered with unique IDs\n   - Support for keyboard shortcuts with user customization\n   - Persistence of key bindings\n\n### Integration Points for Focus Mode\n\nThe Focus Mode feature requires integration at multiple layers:\n\n1. **Settings Layer**: Add `focus_mode_enabled` boolean to persist state\n2. **Event Layer**: Define `FOCUS_MODE_CHANGED` event type\n3. **Command Layer**: Create `ToggleFocusModeCommand` command\n4. **Menu Layer**: Add menu item in View menu\n5. **Module Layer**: Subscribe Chat and Dashboard ViewModels to the event\n\n## Implementation (For Code Modification Tasks)\n\nSince the project files are read-only, below are the complete code changes that would be implemented:\n\n### 1. Modules should\n    subscribe to this event to adjust their UI and behavior accordingly.\n    \"\"\"\n    enabled: bool\n    changed_at: datetime = field(\n        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n    )\n```\n\nAlso add to `__all__`:\n```python\n__all__ = [\n    \"EventTopic\",\n    \"Event\",\n    \"EventRegistry\",\n    \"event\",\n    \"PayloadT\",\n    \"FocusModeChanged\",  # Add this\n]\n```\n\nAnd add assertion at the end:\n```python\nassert _registry.is_known(\"settings.focus_mode.changed\")\n```\n\n### 3. Modify DashboardWidget to De-emphasize Activity Feed\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/modules/dashboard/view/dashboard_widget.py\n// Add in __init__ after signal connections (around line 257):\n\n        self._vm.focusModeChanged.connect(self._on_focus_mode_changed)\n```\n\nAdd handler method:\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/modules/dashboard/view/dashboard_widget.py\n// Add after _apply_theme method (around line 421):\n\n    @Slot(bool)\n    def _on_focus_mode_changed(self, is_deemphasized: bool) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        De-emphasize or restore the dashboard when Focus Mode changes.\n\n        When Focus Mode is enabled, the dashboard is greyed out and has\n        reduced opacity to minimize visual distractions.\n        \"\"\"\n        if is_deemphasized:\n            # Apply de-emphasis styling\n            self.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n                #flockdeskDashboard {\n                    opacity: 0.5;\n                }\n                #flockdeskDashboard > QWidget {\n                    background-color: rgba(128, 128, 128, 0.3);\n                }\n            \"\"\")\n            # Disable interaction with cards\n            for i in range(self._grid.count()):\n                widget = self._grid.itemAt(i).widget()\n                if widget:\n                    widget.setEnabled(False)\n\n            logger.debug(\"Dashboard de-emphasized due to Focus Mode\")\n        else:\n            # Restore normal styling\n            self.setStyleSheet(\"\")\n            # Re-enable interaction\n            for i in range(self._grid.count()):\n                widget = self._grid.itemAt(i).widget()\n                if widget:\n                    widget.setEnabled(True)\n\n            logger.debug(\"Dashboard restored from Focus Mode\")\n\n        self.update()\n```\n\n## Summary\n\n- **Primary finding**: The Focus Mode feature requires modifications across 8 files following the application's established Event-Driven, MVVM, and Service Layer patterns.\n\n- **Key components**:\n  - `EventBus` (`/app/project/flockdesk/core/ipc/event_bus.py`) - Central pub/sub for decoupled communication\n  - `event_types.py` - Event type definitions with `@event` decorator\n  - `SettingsService` (`/app/project/flockdesk/core/services/settings_service.py`) - Persistent settings management\n  - `ShortcutCommandRegistry` (`/app/project/flockdesk/core/shortcuts/commands.py`) - Command pattern implementation\n  - `MenuBar` (`/app/project/flockdesk/core/shell/menu_bar.py`) - Application menu with View menu\n  - `ChatViewModel` / `ChatWidget` - MVVM implementation for Chat module\n  - `DashboardViewModel` / `DashboardWidget` - MVVM implementation for Dashboard module\n\n- **Architectural pattern**:\n  - **Event-Driven Architecture**: Uses `EventBus.publish()` and `EventBus.subscribe()` for loose coupling\n  - **MVVM Pattern**: ViewModels expose Qt Signals, Views bind to them via `connect()`\n  - **Service Layer**: `SettingsService` manages persistent state with namespace-based schemas\n  - **Command Pattern**: `ShortcutCommandRegistry` encapsulates actions with keyboard shortcuts\n\n- **Recommendations**:\n  1. Define the `FocusModeChanged` event payload as a frozen dataclass following existing patterns\n  2. Create a self-contained `ToggleFocusModeCommand` that accesses `SettingsService` to toggle state and `EventBus` to publish changes\n  3. In ViewModels, subscribe to `settings.focus_mode.changed` event and expose state via Qt Signal\n  4. In Views, connect to ViewModel signals to update UI (show snooze indicator, apply de-emphasis styling)\n  5. EventBus Architecture\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/core/ipc/event_bus.py\n// Lines: 276-408\n\nclass EventBus:\n    \"\"\"\n    Public entry point used by every micro-front-end.\n\n    Processes talk to a shared broker, threads inside the same process share\n    exactly one :class:`EventBus` instance (thread-safe singleton).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def subscribe(self, topic: str, handler: Handler) -> _Subscription:\n        \"\"\"\n        Register *handler* for the given *topic* pattern.\n\n        Wildcards: Use ``'*'`` to match one segment, ``'**'`` to match\n        anything recursively (see :pymod:`fnmatch`).\n        \"\"\"\n        # ... implementation\n\n    def publish(self, topic: str, payload: Any, headers: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Fire-and-forget publish.  Safe to call from **any** thread—will enqueue\n        the event into the broker's *inbound* queue.\n        \"\"\"\n        evt = Event(topic=topic, payload=payload, headers=headers or {})\n        self._inbound.put_nowait(evt)\n```\n\n### 2. Event Types Pattern\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/core/ipc/event_types.py\n// Lines: 351-362\n\n@event(topic=EventTopic.SETTINGS, name=\"changed\")\n@dataclass(slots=True, frozen=True)\nclass SettingsChanged(_PayloadBase):\n    user_id: str\n    key: str\n    old_value: Any\n    new_value: Any\n    changed_at: datetime = field(\n        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n    )\n```\n\n### 3. SettingsService Pattern\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/core/services/settings_service.py\n// Lines: 231-258\n\ndef set_value(self, namespace: str, key: str, value: Any) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Update a single field in the namespace and enqueue the change for\n    persistence and event-bus broadcast.\n    \"\"\"\n    with self._lock:\n        meta = self._schemas.get(namespace)\n        instance = self._instances.get(namespace)\n        # ... validation ...\n        setattr(instance, key, value)\n        self._mark_dirty(namespace)\n```\n\n### 4. The bus supports:\n   - Topic-based routing with wildcard matching (`fnmatch`)\n   - Both sync and async handlers\n   - Process-safe communication via a broker process\n   - Request/reply pattern with correlation IDs\n\n2. **MVVM Pattern**: Each module follows MVVM:\n   - **Model**: Domain data classes (e.g., `ChatMessage`, `DashboardWidget`)\n   - **ViewModel**: Qt QObject with signals for UI binding (e.g., `ChatViewModel`, `DashboardViewModel`)\n   - **View**: Qt widgets that connect to ViewModel signals (e.g., `ChatWidget`, `DashboardWidget`)\n\n3. **Service Layer**: Core services like `SettingsService` handle cross-cutting concerns:\n   - Namespace-based settings with dataclass schemas\n   - Automatic persistence with atomic JSON writes\n   - Event-bus broadcast on changes\n\n4. **Command Pattern**: The `ShortcutCommandRegistry` encapsulates actions as first-class objects:\n   - Commands are registered with unique IDs\n   - Support for keyboard shortcuts with user customization\n   - Persistence of key bindings\n\n### Integration Points for Focus Mode\n\nThe Focus Mode feature requires integration at multiple layers:\n\n1. **Settings Layer**: Add `focus_mode_enabled` boolean to persist state\n2. **Event Layer**: Define `FOCUS_MODE_CHANGED` event type\n3. **Command Layer**: Create `ToggleFocusModeCommand` command\n4. **Menu Layer**: Add menu item in View menu\n5. **Module Layer**: Subscribe Chat and Dashboard ViewModels to the event\n\n## Implementation (For Code Modification Tasks)\n\nSince the project files are read-only, below are the complete code changes that would be implemented:\n\n### 1. Safe to call from **any** thread—will enqueue\n    46→        the event into the broker's *inbound* queue.\n    47→        \"\"\"\n    48→        evt = Event(topic=topic, payload=payload, headers=headers or {})\n    49→        self._inbound.put_nowait(evt)\n    50→```\n    51→\n    52→### 2. The bus supports:\n   184→   - Topic-based routing with wildcard matching (`fnmatch`)\n   185→   - Both sync and async handlers\n   186→   - Process-safe communication via a broker process\n   187→   - Request/reply pattern with correlation IDs\n   188→\n   189→2. **MVVM Pattern**: Each module follows MVVM:\n   190→   - **Model**: Domain data classes (e.g., `ChatMessage`, `DashboardWidget`)\n   191→   - **ViewModel**: Qt QObject with signals for UI binding (e.g., `ChatViewModel`, `DashboardViewModel`)\n   192→   - **View**: Qt widgets that connect to ViewModel signals (e.g., `ChatWidget`, `DashboardWidget`)\n   193→\n   194→3. **Service Layer**: Core services like `SettingsService` handle cross-cutting concerns:\n   195→   - Namespace-based settings with dataclass schemas\n   196→   - Automatic persistence with atomic JSON writes\n   197→   - Event-bus broadcast on changes\n   198→\n   199→4. **Command Pattern**: The `ShortcutCommandRegistry` encapsulates actions as first-class objects:\n   200→   - Commands are registered with unique IDs\n   201→   - Support for keyboard shortcuts with user customization\n   202→   - Persistence of key bindings\n   203→\n   204→### Integration Points for Focus Mode\n   205→\n   206→The Focus Mode feature requires integration at multiple layers:\n   207→\n   208→1. **Settings Layer**: Add `focus_mode_enabled` boolean to persist state\n   209→2. **Event Layer**: Define `FOCUS_MODE_CHANGED` event type\n   210→3. **Command Layer**: Create `ToggleFocusModeCommand` command\n   211→4. **Menu Layer**: Add menu item in View menu\n   212→5. **Module Layer**: Subscribe Chat and Dashboard ViewModels to the event\n   213→\n   214→## Implementation (For Code Modification Tasks)\n   215→\n   216→Since the project files are read-only, below are the complete code changes that would be implemented:\n   217→\n   218→### 1. EventBus Architecture\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/core/ipc/event_bus.py\n// Lines: 276-408\n\nclass EventBus:\n    \"\"\"\n    Public entry point used by every micro-front-end.\n\n    Processes talk to a shared broker, threads inside the same process share\n    exactly one :class:`EventBus` instance (thread-safe singleton).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def subscribe(self, topic: str, handler: Handler) -> _Subscription:\n        \"\"\"\n        Register *handler* for the given *topic* pattern.\n\n        Wildcards: Use ``'*'`` to match one segment, ``'**'`` to match\n        anything recursively (see :pymod:`fnmatch`).\n        \"\"\"\n        # ... implementation\n\n    def publish(self, topic: str, payload: Any, headers: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Fire-and-forget publish.  Safe to call from **any** thread—will enqueue\n        the event into the broker's *inbound* queue.\n        \"\"\"\n        evt = Event(topic=topic, payload=payload, headers=headers or {})\n        self._inbound.put_nowait(evt)\n```\n\n### 2. Event Types Pattern\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/core/ipc/event_types.py\n// Lines: 351-362\n\n@event(topic=EventTopic.SETTINGS, name=\"changed\")\n@dataclass(slots=True, frozen=True)\nclass SettingsChanged(_PayloadBase):\n    user_id: str\n    key: str\n    old_value: Any\n    new_value: Any\n    changed_at: datetime = field(\n        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n    )\n```\n\n### 3. SettingsService Pattern\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/core/services/settings_service.py\n// Lines: 231-258\n\ndef set_value(self, namespace: str, key: str, value: Any) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Update a single field in the namespace and enqueue the change for\n    persistence and event-bus broadcast.\n    \"\"\"\n    with self._lock:\n        meta = self._schemas.get(namespace)\n        instance = self._instances.get(namespace)\n        # ... validation ...\n        setattr(instance, key, value)\n        self._mark_dirty(namespace)\n```\n\n### 4. Dashboard ViewModel MVVM Pattern\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/modules/dashboard/viewmodel/dashboard_vm.py\n// Lines: 97-145\n\nclass DashboardViewModel(QObject):\n    \"\"\"\n    Exposes reactive Qt signals + imperative command API for the dashboard.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # ---------------------------- Qt-Signals --------------------------------#\n    widgetsChanged = Signal(list)          # List[DashboardWidget]\n    busyChanged = Signal(bool)             # bool\n    themeChanged = Signal(str)             # str\n    errorOccurred = Signal(str)            # str\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        *,\n        event_bus: EventBus,\n        plugin_manager: PluginManager,\n        settings: Settings,\n        # ...\n    ) -> None:\n        super().__init__(parent=parent)\n        self._event_bus = event_bus\n        # ...\n        self._register_event_listeners()\n```\n\n## Analysis\n\n### Architectural Patterns Identified\n\n1. **Event-Driven Architecture**: The application uses a central `EventBus` for decoupled communication between modules. The bus supports:\n   - Topic-based routing with wildcard matching (`fnmatch`)\n   - Both sync and async handlers\n   - Process-safe communication via a broker process\n   - Request/reply pattern with correlation IDs\n\n2. **MVVM Pattern**: Each module follows MVVM:\n   - **Model**: Domain data classes (e.g., `ChatMessage`, `DashboardWidget`)\n   - **ViewModel**: Qt QObject with signals for UI binding (e.g., `ChatViewModel`, `DashboardViewModel`)\n   - **View**: Qt widgets that connect to ViewModel signals (e.g., `ChatWidget`, `DashboardWidget`)\n\n3. **Service Layer**: Core services like `SettingsService` handle cross-cutting concerns:\n   - Namespace-based settings with dataclass schemas\n   - Automatic persistence with atomic JSON writes\n   - Event-bus broadcast on changes\n\n4. **Command Pattern**: The `ShortcutCommandRegistry` encapsulates actions as first-class objects:\n   - Commands are registered with unique IDs\n   - Support for keyboard shortcuts with user customization\n   - Persistence of key bindings\n\n### Integration Points for Focus Mode\n\nThe Focus Mode feature requires integration at multiple layers:\n\n1. **Settings Layer**: Add `focus_mode_enabled` boolean to persist state\n2. **Event Layer**: Define `FOCUS_MODE_CHANGED` event type\n3. **Command Layer**: Create `ToggleFocusModeCommand` command\n4. **Menu Layer**: Add menu item in View menu\n5. **Module Layer**: Subscribe Chat and Dashboard ViewModels to the event\n\n## Implementation (For Code Modification Tasks)\n\nSince the project files are read-only, below are the complete code changes that would be implemented:\n\n### 1. For unit-testing we only need ‘publish’.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self) -> None:\n        self._messages: List[tuple[str, dict]] = []\n\n    # Production code normally awaits/awaits – keep sync for simplicity.\n    def publish(self, topic: str, payload: dict | None = None) -> None:  # noqa: D401\n        self._messages.append((topic, payload or {}))\n\n    # Helper assertion API so we have readable failure output.\n    def pop(self, topic: str) -> dict:\n        if not self._messages:\n            pytest.fail(f\"No messages on bus – expected at least “{topic}”.\")\n        t, payload = self._messages.pop(0)\n        assert (\n            t == topic\n        ), f\"First message was topic={t!r} not expected {topic!r}.  Payload={payload!r}\"\n        return payload\n\n    def topics(self) -> list[str]:\n        return [t for t, _ in self._messages]\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# Fixtures                                                                    \n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n\n@pytest.fixture()\ndef config_dir(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> Path:\n    \"\"\"\n    Ensure every test receives an isolated configuration directory.\n\n    SettingsService relies on XDG_CONFIG_HOME for disk persistence.\n    \"\"\"\n    path = tmp_path / \".config\"\n    path.mkdir()\n    monkeypatch.setenv(\"XDG_CONFIG_HOME\", str(path))\n    return path\n\n\n@pytest.fixture()\ndef event_bus() -> _RecorderBus:\n    return _RecorderBus()\n\n\n@pytest.fixture()\ndef settings_service(\n    event_bus: _RecorderBus, config_dir: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch\n):\n    \"\"\"\n    Return a fully-wired SettingsService that writes into *config_dir* and\n    publishes onto the in-memory event bus.\n\n    We patch-inject our in-mem bus on construction.
